var __defProp = Object.defineProperty;
var __getOwnPropNames = Object.getOwnPropertyNames;
var __esm = (fn, res, err) => function __init() {
  if (err) throw err[0];
  try {
    return fn && (res = (0, fn[__getOwnPropNames(fn)[0]])(fn = 0)), res;
  } catch (e) {
    throw err = [e], e;
  }
};
var __export = (target, all) => {
  for (var name in all)
    __defProp(target, name, { get: all[name], enumerable: true });
};

var GraphCheckpointService;
var init_graph_checkpoint_service = __esm({
  "packages/cli/src/graph-checkpoint-service.ts"() {
    "use strict";
    GraphCheckpointService = class {
      constructor(options) {
        this.options = options;
        for (const [connection, binding] of options.bindings) this.register(connection, binding);
      }
      options;
      bindings = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      closed = false;
      retired = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakSet();
      register(connection, binding) {
        this.assertOpen();
        if (this.retired.has(connection)) throw new Error("CONNECTION_RETIRED");
        if (this.bindings.has(connection)) throw new Error("ALREADY_REGISTERED");
        if (typeof binding.authorize !== "function") throw new Error("UNAUTHENTICATED");
        const { authorize, ...data } = binding;
        this.bindings.set(connection, { ...structuredClone(data), authorize });
      }
      unregister(connection) {
        this.bindings.delete(connection);
        this.retired.add(connection);
      }
      close() {
        this.closed = true;
      }
      async execute(connection, value) {
        this.assertOpen();
        const binding = this.bindings.get(connection);
        if (!binding) throw new Error("UNAUTHENTICATED");
        const request2 = parseGraphCheckpointRequest(value);
        if (request2.threadId !== binding.graphRunId) throw new Error("RUN_MISMATCH");
        const authorize = binding.authorize;
        const decision = await authorize();
        if (decision !== void 0) throw new Error("POLICY_DENIED: authorization must resolve without a decision payload or throw");
        this.assertOpen();
        if (this.bindings.get(connection) !== binding) throw new Error("UNAUTHENTICATED");
        const run2 = this.options.runs.get(binding.scope, binding.graphRunId);
        if (!run2) throw new Error("RUN_NOT_FOUND");
        const result = this.options.checkpoints.execute({
          scope: binding.scope,
          graphRunId: binding.graphRunId,
          expectedGeneration: binding.generation,
          expectedRevision: run2.revision,
          expectedOwner: binding.owner,
          identity: binding.identity,
          now: this.options.now?.() ?? Date.now()
        }, request2);
        return result.response;
      }
      assertOpen() {
        if (this.closed) throw new Error("CHECKPOINT_SERVICE_CLOSED");
      }
    };
  }
});

function snapshot(value) {
  const copy = structuredClone(value);
  const freeze = (item) => {
    if (item !== null && typeof item === "object") {
      Object.values(item).forEach(freeze);
      Object.freeze(item);
    }
  };
  freeze(copy);
  return copy;
}
function canonical(value) {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
  return `{${Object.keys(value).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical(value[key])}`).join(",")}}`;
}
function graphError(code, message) {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    code,
    message: message.slice(0, 4096) || code,
    retryable: false,
    nextAction: code === "ACTION_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" || code === "CANCEL_UNCONFIRMED" ? "reconcile" : "stop"
  };
}
function errorCode(error) {
  if (error instanceof Denied) return error.code;
  const message = String(error);
  if (/GRANT_EXPIRED/.test(message)) return "GRANT_EXPIRED";
  if (/STALE_GENERATION|OWNER_MISMATCH|LEASE_EXPIRED/.test(message)) return "STALE_GENERATION";
  if (/PROGRAM_IDENTITY/.test(message)) return "CHECKPOINT_INCOMPATIBLE";
  if (/UNRESOLVED|RETRY_NOT_SAFE/.test(message)) return "ACTION_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN";
  if (/BUDGET|ACCOUNTING/.test(message)) return "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED";
  if (/REVISION_CONFLICT/.test(message)) return "REVISION_CONFLICT";
  return "POLICY_DENIED";
}

          isAvailable: original?.isAvailable ?? (async () => ({ available: true, last_checked_at: 0 })),
          invoke: (task, invocationSignal) => {
            return (async function* () {
              await policy?.(binding.workspace.workspace_path);
              if (invocationSignal.aborted) throw new Denied("POLICY_DENIED", "execution cancelled before invocation");
              beforeEffect(task.run_id);
              invocationRunId = task.run_id;
              try {
                if (original) {
                  for await (const event of original.invoke(task, invocationSignal)) {
                    const payload = event.type === "usage.updated" || event.type === "usage.final" ? event.payload : event.type === "done" && event.payload && typeof event.payload === "object" ? event.payload.usage : void 0;
                    if (payload !== void 0) {
                      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) throw new Error("invalid adapter usage payload");
                      const usage2 = payload;
                      let tokens = 0;
                      for (const field of ["tokens_in", "tokens_out"]) {
                        const amount = usage2[field];
                        if (amount !== void 0) {
                          if (typeof amount !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount < 0) throw new Error("invalid adapter token usage");
                          cumulativeUsage[field] = Math.max(cumulativeUsage[field], amount);
                        }
                      }
                      tokens = cumulativeUsage["tokens_in"] + cumulativeUsage["tokens_out"];
                      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(tokens)) throw new Error("invalid adapter total usage");
                      observeTokens(tokens);
                      const cost = usage2["cost_usd"];
                      if (cost !== void 0 && (typeof cost !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(cost) || cost < 0)) throw new Error("invalid adapter cost usage");
                    }
                    yield event;
                  }
                  return;
                }
                if (execution.kind !== "command") throw new Error("invalid execution registration");
                command = await runShellCommand(execution.command, {
                  cwd: binding.workspace.workspace_path,
                  timeoutMs: 12e4,
                  outputLimit: 16e3,
                  ...invocationSignal ? { signal: invocationSignal } : {}
                });
                yield {
                  event_id: randomUUID(),
                  run_id: task.run_id,
                  session_id: task.session_id,
                  seq: 1,
                  type: "done",
                  created_at: Date.now() / 1e3,
                  terminal: true,
                  payload: { status: command.exitCode === 0 ? "completed" : "failed", last_seq: 0 }
                };
              } catch (error) {
                threw = true;
                throw error;
              }
            })();
          },
          confirmStopped: async (runId) => original?.confirmStopped ? original.confirmStopped(runId) : { stopped: command?.termination?.stop_confirmed === true, evidence: command?.termination?.evidence ?? "no trusted stop receipt" }
        };
        const candidate = await executeCandidateImplementation({
          plan: { ...execution.plan, agent_id: wrapper.id },
          workspace: binding.workspace,
          adapters: [wrapper],
          signal,
          cancellationGraceMs: 50,
          authorize: async () => {
            this.authorize(binding);
            this.fence(binding);
            return { allow: true };
          }
        });
        if (!invocationRunId) throw new Denied("POLICY_DENIED", candidate.reason ?? "candidate preflight refused");
        if (threw || candidate.status === "execution_unconfirmed" || candidate.execution?.failure_code) throw new Error("adapter result or execution unconfirmed");
        let stopTimer;
        const stopped2 = await Promise.race([
          wrapper.confirmStopped(invocationRunId),
          new Promise((resolve4) => {
            stopTimer = setTimeout(() => resolve4({ stopped: false }), 1e3);
          })
        ]).finally(() => {
          if (stopTimer) clearTimeout(stopTimer);
        });
        if (stopped2.stopped !== true || !stopped2.evidence?.trim()) throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed");
        const usage = candidate.execution;
        if ([usage?.tokens_in, usage?.tokens_out].some((value) => value !== void 0 && (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0))) throw new Error("invalid adapter usage");
        return { status: candidate.status === "implementation_complete" ? "completed" : "failed", candidate, ...command ? { command } : {} };
      }
    };
  }
});

function canonical2(v) {
  if (v === null || typeof v !== "object") return JSON.stringify(v);
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical2).join(",")}]`;
  const r = v;
  return `{${Object.keys(r).sort().map((k) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical2(r[k])}`).join(",")}}`;
}

function deferred() {
  let resolve4;
  let reject;
  const promise = new Promise((res, rej) => {
    resolve4 = res;
    reject = rej;
  });
  void promise.catch(() => {
  });

function boundedOption(value, fallback, max) {
  const result = value ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(result) || result < 1 || result > max) throw new Error("WORKER_OPTION_INVALID");
  return result;
}

  }
}

        };

          result: result.promise,
          exit: exit.promise,
          cancel,
          stop: (reason) => {
            cancel(reason);

          },
          logs: () => ({ stdout: stdout.toString("utf8"), stderr: stderr.toString("utf8") })
        });
        const spawnFailed = (error) => {

            cancel(String(error));
            return;
          }
          observedExit = true;
          record.active = false;
          abort.abort();
          clear();

          });

              graphRunId: binding.graphRunId,
              generation: binding.generation,

              pid: child2.pid,

            });
          });
          child2.stdout.on("error", (error) => cancel(String(error)));
          child2.stderr.on("error", (error) => cancel(String(error)));
          child2.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
            stdout = append(stdout, chunk);
          });
          child2.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {

          });

          child2.once("spawn", () => {

                programId,
                source: program.source,

                requestTimeoutMs
              });
            } catch (error) {
              cancel(String(error));
            }
          });
          monitor = setInterval(() => {
            if (stopping || observedExit) return;
            try {
              assertActive();
              if (!didReady && Date.now() - startedAt > startupMs) cancel("WORKER_STARTUP_TIMEOUT");
              else if (Date.now() - lastHeartbeat > heartbeatMs) cancel("WORKER_HEARTBEAT_EXPIRED");
            } catch (error) {
              cancel(String(error));
            }
          }, Math.max(10, Math.min(100, Math.floor(heartbeatMs / 3))));
        } catch (error) {
          spawnFailed(error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
        }
        return handle2;
      }
    };

// packages/cli/src/adapter-lifecycle.ts
async function* boundedAdapterEvents(events, signal) {
  const iterator = events[Symbol.asyncIterator]();
  let timer;
  let cancellationError;
  let waiting;
  const onAbort = () => {
    timer ??= setTimeout(() => {
      cancellationError = new Error("adapter cancellation grace elapsed");
      waiting?.(cancellationError);
    }, 1e3);
  };
  const nextEvent = () => new Promise((resolve4, reject) => {
    if (cancellationError) {
      reject(cancellationError);
      return;
    }
    waiting = reject;
    Promise.resolve().then(() => iterator.next()).then(
      (value) => {
        waiting = void 0;
        resolve4(value);
      },
      (error) => {
        waiting = void 0;
        reject(error);
      }
    );
  });
  signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
  if (signal.aborted) onAbort();
  let exhausted = false;
  try {
    while (true) {
      const next = await nextEvent();
      if (next.done) {
        exhausted = true;
        return;
      }
      yield next.value;
    }
  } finally {
    waiting = void 0;
    signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!exhausted) void Promise.resolve().then(() => iterator.return?.()).catch(() => {
    });
  }
}
async function confirmAdapterStopped(adapter, runId) {
  if (!adapter.confirmStopped) return false;
  let timer;
  try {
    const proof = await Promise.race([
      Promise.resolve().then(() => adapter.confirmStopped(runId)),
      new Promise((resolve4) => {
        timer = setTimeout(() => resolve4(void 0), 1e3);
      })
    ]);
    return proof?.stopped === true && typeof proof.evidence === "string" && proof.evidence.trim().length > 0;
  } catch {
    return false;
  } finally {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

import { join } from "node:path";
import { acquireRepoWriteLock, FifoSemaphore, SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError } from "@osstack/autonomy";
import { resolveRepositoryScope } from "@osstack/core";
var GovernedExecution = class {
  constructor(options) {
    this.options = options;
  }
  options;
  activeRuns = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  quarantinedRepositories = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  quarantine(repoId, runId) {
    const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(repoId) ?? /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    owners.add(runId);
    this.quarantinedRepositories.set(repoId, owners);
  }
  get hasWriters() {
    return this.options.writes.in_use > 0;
  }
  async acquire(input) {
    const check2 = async () => {
      if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
      if (input.kind === "write") await this.options.checkHealth();
      await input.authorize?.();
    };
    await check2();
    const scope = input.workspacePath === void 0 ? void 0 : await resolveRepositoryScope(input.workspacePath, input.repoId === void 0 ? {} : { repoId: input.repoId });
    if (input.kind === "write" && !scope) throw new Error("trusted repository scope required for writes");
    if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
    if (this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) throw new Error("run already admitted or queued");
    this.activeRuns.add(input.runId);
    let local;
    let global;
    let lock;
    let queue;
    const releasePermits = () => {
      global?.release();
      local?.release();
      this.activeRuns.delete(input.runId);
      if (scope && queue?.in_use === 0 && queue.pending === 0) this.options.repositories.delete(scope.repoId);
    };
    try {
      if (input.kind === "write" && scope) {
        queue = this.options.repositories.get(scope.repoId) ?? new FifoSemaphore(1);
        this.options.repositories.set(scope.repoId, queue);
        local = await queue.acquire(input.signal);
      }
      global = await (input.kind === "read" ? this.options.reads : this.options.writes).acquire(input.signal);
      if (input.kind === "write" && scope) {
        lock = await acquireRepoWriteLock({ root_dir: join(scope.commonDir, "osstack", "writer-locks"), recover_stale: false, repo_id: scope.repoId, autonomy_run_id: input.runId });
      }
      if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
      await check2();
    } catch (error) {
      try {
        await lock?.release();
      } finally {
        releasePermits();
      }
      throw error;
    }
    let released = false;
    let retained = false;
    let renewing = Promise.resolve();
    const timer = lock === void 0 ? void 0 : setInterval(() => {
      renewing = renewing.then(async () => {
        if (!released) await lock.renew();
      }).catch((error) => {
        retained = true;
        if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
        process.stderr.write(`repository lease renewal failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}
`);
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
      });
    }, 2e4);
    timer?.unref();
    let reconciled;
    return {
      assertActive: async () => {
        if (released || retained) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
        await lock?.assertActive();
        if (released || retained) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
      },
      releaseAfterReconciliation: (verify) => {
        if (released) return Promise.resolve();
        if (reconciled) return reconciled;
        if (typeof verify !== "function") return Promise.reject(new Error("trusted reconciliation verifier required"));
        reconciled = (async () => {
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          await renewing;
          const result = await verify();
          if (result !== void 0) throw new Error("invalid reconciliation authority");
          await lock?.release();
          released = true;
          retained = false;
          if (scope) {
            const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
            owners?.delete(input.runId);
            if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
          }
          releasePermits();
        })().catch((error) => {
          retained = true;
          if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          throw error;
        }).finally(() => {
          reconciled = void 0;
        });
        return reconciled;
      },
      retain: (reason) => {
        retained = true;
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
        if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
        process.stderr.write(`osstack: retaining write lease ${input.runId}: ${reason}
`);
      },
      release: async () => {
        if (released) return;
        if (retained) throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release");
        released = true;
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
        await renewing;
        if (retained) {
          released = false;
          throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release");
        }
        try {
          await lock?.release();
        } catch (error) {
          released = false;
          retained = true;
          if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          process.stderr.write(`repository lock release failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}
`);
          throw error;
        }
        releasePermits();
      }
    };
  }
};

// packages/cli/src/control-plane-guard.ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, realpathSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { homedir } from "node:os";
import { basename, dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
var ControlPlaneGuardError = class extends Error {
  code;
  constructor(code, msg) {
    super(msg);
    this.name = "ControlPlaneGuardError";
    this.code = code;
  }
};
var expand = (p) => {
  const lexical = resolve(p.replace(/^~(?=$|\/)/, homedir()));
  let probe = lexical;
  const segments = [];
  while (!existsSync(probe)) {
    const parent = resolve(probe, "..");
    if (parent === probe) return lexical;
    segments.unshift(basename(probe));
    probe = parent;
  }
  try {
    return resolve(realpathSync(probe), ...segments);
  } catch {
    return lexical;
  }
};
function defaultOsstackHome() {
  return resolve(homedir(), ".osstack");
}
function loadRoleFile(checkoutRoot) {
  const file = resolve(checkoutRoot, "control-plane.json");
  if (!existsSync(file)) return null;
  let raw;
  try {
    raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
  } catch (err) {
    throw new ControlPlaneGuardError("invalid_role_file", `control-plane.json is not valid JSON: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  }
  const r = raw;
  if (r === null || typeof r !== "object" || r.role !== "stable-serving" && r.role !== "candidate" && r.role !== "dev" || !Array.isArray(r.allowed_osstack_homes) || r.allowed_osstack_homes.some((h) => typeof h !== "string" || h.length === 0)) {
    throw new ControlPlaneGuardError("invalid_role_file", "control-plane.json must declare role (stable-serving|candidate|dev) and allowed_osstack_homes (non-empty strings)");
  }
  if (r.schema !== void 0) {
    const s = r.schema;
    if (!Number.isInteger(s.min) || !Number.isInteger(s.max) || s.min > s.max) {
      throw new ControlPlaneGuardError("invalid_role_file", "control-plane.json schema must be { min <= max } integers");
    }
  }
  return { role: r.role, allowed_osstack_homes: [...r.allowed_osstack_homes], ...r.schema ? { schema: { min: r.schema.min, max: r.schema.max } } : {} };
}
function homeIsReserved(home) {
  return existsSync(resolve(expand(home), "control-plane-reserved.json"));
}
function assertServeAllowed(opts) {
  const defaultHome = expand(opts.defaultHome ?? defaultOsstackHome());
  const home = expand(opts.osstackHome);
  const declared = loadRoleFile(opts.checkoutRoot);
  if (declared === null) {
    if (home !== defaultHome && homeIsReserved(home)) {
      throw new ControlPlaneGuardError(
        "home_not_allowed",
        "the requested OSSTACK_HOME is RESERVED (control-plane-reserved.json present); serving it requires a declared role file that allows it"
      );
    }
    if (home === defaultHome) {
      throw new ControlPlaneGuardError(
        "undeclared_checkout_on_default_home",
        "this checkout has no control-plane.json role declaration and may NOT serve the default (stable) OSSTACK_HOME; point OSSTACK_HOME at a dev/test home, or deploy this checkout properly (the deploy step writes the untracked role file)"
      );
    }
    return { role: "undeclared-dev", homeCategory: "custom" };
  }
  const allowed = declared.allowed_osstack_homes.map(expand);
  if (!allowed.includes(home)) {
    throw new ControlPlaneGuardError(
      "home_not_allowed",
      `this checkout's control-plane.json (role=${declared.role}) does not allow the requested OSSTACK_HOME`
    );
  }
  if (home === defaultHome && declared.role !== "stable-serving") {
    throw new ControlPlaneGuardError(
      "home_not_allowed",
      `role ${declared.role} may not serve the default (stable) OSSTACK_HOME; only stable-serving may`
    );
  }
  return {
    role: declared.role,
    homeCategory: home === defaultHome ? "default-stable" : "declared",
    ...declared.schema ? { schema: declared.schema } : {}
  };
}
function assertDbAccessAllowed(opts) {
  try {
    return assertServeAllowed(opts);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof ControlPlaneGuardError) {
      throw new ControlPlaneGuardError(err.code, `refusing to open the control-plane DB \u2014 ${err.message}`);
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

// packages/cli/src/guarded-open-db.ts
function guardedOpenDb(opts) {
  let checkoutRoot = dirname2(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  try {
    checkoutRoot = dirname2(realpathSync2(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)));
  } catch {
  }
  const home = dirname2(opts.path);
  assertDbAccessAllowed({ checkoutRoot, osstackHome: home });
  return coreOpenDb(opts);
}

// packages/cli/src/rpc-latency.ts
var RING_CAPACITY = 512;
var MAX_METHODS = 64;
var OVERFLOW_METHOD = "(other)";
var RpcLatencyRecorder = class {
  windows = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  record(method, durationMs) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(durationMs) || durationMs < 0) return;
    let w = this.windows.get(method);
    if (w === void 0 && this.windows.size >= MAX_METHODS) {
      method = OVERFLOW_METHOD;
      w = this.windows.get(method);
    }
    if (w === void 0) {
      w = {
        samples: new Float64Array(RING_CAPACITY),
        cursor: 0,
        filled: false,
        total: 0,
        maxMs: 0
      };
      this.windows.set(method, w);
    }
    w.samples[w.cursor] = durationMs;
    w.cursor += 1;
    if (w.cursor === RING_CAPACITY) {
      w.cursor = 0;
      w.filled = true;
    }
    w.total += 1;
    if (durationMs > w.maxMs) w.maxMs = durationMs;
  }
  /** Sorted by descending p95 so the top offender reads first. */
  snapshot() {
    const out = [];
    for (const [method, w] of this.windows) {
      const size = w.filled ? RING_CAPACITY : w.cursor;
      if (size === 0) continue;
      const window = Array.from(w.samples.subarray(0, size)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      out.push({
        method,
        count: w.total,
        window: size,
        p50_ms: percentile(window, 0.5),
        p95_ms: percentile(window, 0.95),
        max_ms: w.maxMs
      });
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => b.p95_ms - a.p95_ms);
    return out;
  }
};
function percentile(sorted, q) {
  if (sorted.length === 0) return 0;
  const rank = Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(q * sorted.length) - 1));
  return sorted[rank];
}

import { execFile } from "node:child_process";

// packages/cli/src/policy-gate.ts
import { loadPolicyProfile } from "@osstack/core";
var OBSERVE_POLICY_DENIED_REASON = "policy_denied: policy mode is observe \u2014 execution refused; see .osstack/policy.yaml";

// packages/cli/src/repository-policy.ts
function makeRepositoryPolicyGate(workspacePath, requirePolicy = false) {
  return async () => {
    try {
      const scope = await resolveRepositoryScope2(workspacePath);
      const env = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(process.env).filter(([key]) => !key.startsWith("GIT_")));
      Object.assign(env, { GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1", GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null", GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: "0" });
      const options = { cwd: scope.workspacePath, env, encoding: "utf8", timeout: 5e3, killSignal: "SIGKILL", maxBuffer: 64 * 1024, shell: false };
      const top = await promisify(execFile)("git", ["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], options);
      const listing = await promisify(execFile)("git", ["worktree", "list", "--porcelain", "-z"], options);
      const first = listing.stdout.split("\0")[0];
      if (!first?.startsWith("worktree ")) throw new Error("primary repository policy root unavailable");
      const primary = await realpath(first.slice("worktree ".length));
      await resolveRepositoryScope2(primary, { repoId: scope.repoId });
      const root2 = await realpath(top.stdout.replace(/\r?\n$/, ""));
      let present = false;
      for (const path of /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([primary, root2, scope.workspacePath])) {
        const profile = await loadPolicyProfile2(path);
        present ||= profile !== void 0;
        if (profile?.mode === "observe") return { allow: false, reason: OBSERVE_POLICY_DENIED_REASON };
      }
      if (requirePolicy && !present) return { allow: false, reason: "graph policy authorization required" };
      return { allow: true };
    } catch (error) {
      return { allow: false, reason: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
    }
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/session-collaborator.ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";

// packages/cli/src/continuation-output-checks.ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
function checkContinuationOutput(text, checks) {
  let value;
  let jsonValid = false;
  try {
    value = JSON.parse(text);
    jsonValid = true;
  } catch {
  }
  const results = checks.map((check2) => {
    if (check2.kind === "text_equals") return text === check2.expected;
    if (!jsonValid) return false;
    let current2 = value;
    for (const key of check2.path) {
      if (typeof current2 !== "object" || current2 === null || !Object.hasOwn(current2, key)) return false;
      current2 = current2[key];
    }
    return current2 === check2.expected;
  });
  return {
    passed: results.length > 0 && results.every(Boolean),
    details: {
      evaluator: "frozen-output-contract-v1",
      output_sha256: createHash("sha256").update(text).digest("hex"),
      results
    }
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/session-collaborator.ts
var TrustStore = z.object({
  schema_version: z.literal(1),
  keys: z.array(z.object({ key_id: z.string().min(1).max(256), public_key_pem: z.string().min(1).max(8192) }).strict()).max(32)
}).strict();
async function readContinuationTrust(path) {
  let text;
  try {
    text = await readFile(path, "utf8");
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ENOENT") return /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    throw error;
  }
  if (text.length > 3e5) throw new Error("continuation trust store is too large");
  const parsed = TrustStore.parse(JSON.parse(text));
  for (const key of parsed.keys) {
    if (!/^\s*-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----[A-Za-z0-9+/=\r\n\t ]+-----END PUBLIC KEY-----\s*$/.test(key.public_key_pem) || createPublicKey(key.public_key_pem).asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519") {
      throw new Error("continuation trust entries must contain only Ed25519 PUBLIC KEY PEM");
    }
  }
  const keys = new Map(parsed.keys.map((key) => [key.key_id, key.public_key_pem]));
  if (keys.size !== parsed.keys.length) throw new Error("duplicate continuation trust key_id");
  return keys;
}
var SessionCollaborator = class {
  constructor(ports) {
    this.ports = ports;
  }
  ports;
  running = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  stopped = false;
  async policy(runtimeId) {
    const adapter = this.ports.adapters.get(runtimeId);
    if (!isControlledSessionAdapter(adapter)) throw new Error("controlled_runtime_required");
    return await adapter.continuationPolicy();
  }
  async install(signed) {
    if (this.stopped || this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("daemon_draining");
    const verified = verifyContinuationGrant(signed, {
      trusted_public_keys: this.ports.trustedPublicKeys,
      now: () => (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
    });
    const policy = await this.policy(verified.body.runtime_id);
    if (policy.fingerprint !== verified.body.runtime_policy_sha256) throw new Error("runtime_policy_mismatch");
    verifyContinuationGrant(signed, { trusted_public_keys: this.ports.trustedPublicKeys, now: () => (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString() });
    if (this.stopped || this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("daemon_draining");
    const grant = this.ports.repository.install(verified.body, verified.digest, verified.key_id, Date.now() / 1e3, verified.signature);
    if (grant.status === "active") this.schedule(grant.body.grant_id);
    return grant;
  }
  revoke(grantId) {
    const grant = this.ports.repository.revoke(grantId, Date.now() / 1e3);
    this.running.get(grantId)?.controller.abort();
    return grant;
  }
  recover() {
    this.ports.repository.recover(Date.now() / 1e3);
    this.resumePending();
  }
  resumePending() {
    for (const grant of this.ports.repository.listActive()) this.schedule(grant.body.grant_id);
  }
  isScheduled(grantId) {
    return !this.stopped && !this.ports.draining() && this.running.has(grantId);
  }
  schedule(grantId) {
    if (this.stopped || this.ports.draining() || this.running.has(grantId)) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const done = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      const repo2 = this.ports.repository;
      while (!this.stopped && !controller.signal.aborted && !this.ports.draining()) {
        const grant = repo2.get(grantId);
        if (grant === void 0 || grant.status !== "active") return;
        if (Date.parse(grant.body.expires_at) <= Date.now()) {
          repo2.block(grantId, "expired", Date.now() / 1e3);
          return;
        }
        const verified = verifyContinuationGrant({ body: grant.body, key_id: grant.key_id, signature: grant.signature }, {
          trusted_public_keys: this.ports.trustedPublicKeys,
          now: () => (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
        });
        if (verified.digest !== grant.digest) throw new Error("grant_digest_mismatch");
        const policy = await this.policy(grant.body.runtime_id);
        if (policy.fingerprint !== grant.body.runtime_policy_sha256) throw new Error("runtime_policy_mismatch");
        if (this.stopped || controller.signal.aborted || this.ports.draining()) return;
        const check2 = repo2.beginCheck(grantId, Date.now() / 1e3);
        if (check2.outcome !== "checking") return;
        const result = checkContinuationOutput(check2.session.assistant_text, check2.step.checks);
        const reservation = repo2.finishCheck(check2.attempt.attempt_id, result, Date.now() / 1e3);
        if (reservation.outcome !== "reserved") return;
        const remainingMs = Math.max(1, Date.parse(grant.body.expires_at) - Date.now());
        const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), Math.min(grant.body.turn_timeout_ms, remainingMs));
        try {
          const response = await this.ports.send(reservation, controller);
          if (!response.ok) {
            repo2.failAttempt(reservation.attempt.attempt_id, response.error?.code ?? "delivery_failed", Date.now() / 1e3);
            return;
          }
        } catch {
          repo2.failAttempt(reservation.attempt.attempt_id, "delivery_uncertain", Date.now() / 1e3);
          return;
        } finally {
          clearTimeout(timer);
        }
      }
    }).catch((error) => {
      const reason = error instanceof Error && /^[a-z_]{1,80}$/.test(error.message) ? error.message : "continuation_failed";
      try {
        this.ports.repository.block(grantId, reason, Date.now() / 1e3);
      } catch {
      }
    }).finally(() => {
      this.running.delete(grantId);
    });
    this.running.set(grantId, { controller, done });
  }
  async stop() {
    this.stopped = true;
    for (const run2 of this.running.values()) run2.controller.abort();
    await Promise.allSettled([...this.running.values()].map((run2) => run2.done));
  }
};

// packages/cli/src/session-wait.ts
import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
function describeSessionWait(input) {
  if (input.applying) return null;
  if (input.pending_message !== void 0) {
    return {
      reason: input.pending_message.state === "accepted" ? "delivery_uncertain" : "delivery_failed",
      message_id: input.pending_message.message_id,
      automatic_continuation: false
    };
  }
  if (input.state !== "idle" && input.state !== "awaiting_decision") return null;
  if (!input.resume_ready) return { reason: "resume_unavailable", automatic_continuation: false };
  if (input.state === "idle") return { reason: "awaiting_instruction", automatic_continuation: false };
  const parsed = SessionDecision.safeParse(input.decision);
  const continuation = parsed.success ? parsed.data.continuation : void 0;
  if (continuation === void 0) return { reason: "unclassified_decision", automatic_continuation: false };
  return {
    reason: continuation.gate_kind,
    request_source: "agent_reported",
    continuation,
    automatic_continuation: false
  };
}

var TERMINAL_STATES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
  "blocked",
  "delivery_evidence_failed",
  "escalated",
  "cancelled",
  "completed"
]);
var DEPLOY_ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
  "production_healthy",
  "production_rolled_back",
  "rollback_failed"
]);
var ROLLBACK_ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
  "production_rolled_back",
  "rollback_failed"

function parsedObject(value) {
  if (value === null) return void 0;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
    return isObject(parsed) ? parsed : void 0;
  } catch {
    return void 0;
  }
}
function isObject(value) {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}
function child(value, key) {
  return isObject(value) ? value[key] : void 0;
}
function finiteNonnegative(value) {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 ? value : void 0;
}
function integerCount(value) {
  const number = finiteNonnegative(value);
  return number !== void 0 && Number.isInteger(number) ? number : 0;
}
function engineContext(row) {
  const attempt = parsedObject(row.attempt_json);
  const context = child(attempt, "engine_context");
  return isObject(context) ? context : {};
}
function riskLevel(row) {
  const post = parsedObject(row.post_diff_risk_json);
  const preflight = parsedObject(row.preflight_risk_json);
  const value = child(post, "level") ?? child(preflight, "level");
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : void 0;
}
function hasHumanDecision(row) {
  const approval = parsedObject(row.approval_json);
  return isObject(child(approval, "decision"));
}
function deployOutcome(row) {
  const deploy = parsedObject(row.deploy_json);
  const value = child(deploy, "outcome");
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : void 0;
}
function ratio(numerator, denominator) {
  return {
    numerator,
    denominator,
    value: denominator === 0 ? 0 : rounded(numerator / denominator)
  };
}
function mean(total, denominator) {
  return {
    total,
    denominator,
    value: denominator === 0 ? 0 : rounded(total / denominator)
  };
}
function rounded(value) {
  return Number(value.toFixed(4));
}
function transitionTelemetry(db2, row) {
  let previousFindings = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  let repeatedFindings = 0;
  let reviewerMutations = 0;
  let unauthorizedActions = 0;
  let adapterUnavailable = 0;
  let recoveryAttempts = 0;
  let recoverySuccesses = 0;
  const transitions = listAutonomyTransitions(
    db2,
    row.autonomy_run_id
  );
  for (const transition of transitions) {
    const event = parsedObject(transition.event_json) ?? {};
    const contextPatch = child(event, "context_patch");
    const localResult = child(contextPatch, "local_result");
    const progress = child(localResult, "progress");
    const rawFindings = child(progress, "finding_fingerprints");
    if (Array.isArray(rawFindings)) {
      const current2 = new Set(
        rawFindings.filter(
          (finding) => typeof finding === "string"
        )
      );
      repeatedFindings += [...current2].filter(
        (finding) => previousFindings.has(finding)
      ).length;
      previousFindings = current2;
    }
    const telemetry = child(localResult, "telemetry") ?? child(event, "telemetry");
    reviewerMutations += integerCount(
      child(telemetry, "reviewer_mutation_count")
    );
    unauthorizedActions += integerCount(
      child(telemetry, "unauthorized_action_count")
    );
    adapterUnavailable += integerCount(
      child(telemetry, "adapter_unavailable_count")
    );
    const recovery2 = child(event, "recovery");
    if (child(recovery2, "kind") === "restart") recoveryAttempts = 1;
  }
  if (recoveryAttempts > 0 && (row.state === "completed" || transitions.some(
    (transition) => child(
      parsedObject(transition.event_json)?.["recovery"],
      "succeeded"
    ) === true
  ))) {
    recoverySuccesses = 1;
  }
  return {
    repeatedFindings,
    reviewerMutations,
    unauthorizedActions,
    adapterUnavailable,
    recoveryAttempts,
    recoverySuccesses
  };
}
function createScorecard(rows, telemetryByRun, window, from, through, activeFreezeCount) {
  const eligibleTerminal = rows.filter(
    (row) => TERMINAL_STATES.has(row.state) && (riskLevel(row) === "R1" || riskLevel(row) === "R2")
  );
  const unattendedCompleted = eligibleTerminal.filter(
    (row) => row.state === "completed" && !hasHumanDecision(row)
  ).length;
  const avoidableHumanInterventions = eligibleTerminal.filter(
    hasHumanDecision
  ).length;
  const builderIterations = rows.map((row) => finiteNonnegative(
    child(engineContext(row), "builder_iterations")
  )).filter((value) => value !== void 0);
  const reviewRounds = rows.map((row) => finiteNonnegative(
    child(engineContext(row), "review_rounds")
  )).filter((value) => value !== void 0);
  let repeatedFindingCount = 0;
  let reviewerMutationCount = 0;
  let unauthorizedActionCount = 0;
  let recoveryAttempts = 0;
  let recoverySuccesses = 0;
  for (const row of rows) {
    const telemetry = telemetryByRun.get(row.autonomy_run_id);
    if (telemetry === void 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `missing scorecard telemetry for ${row.autonomy_run_id}`
      );
    }
    repeatedFindingCount += telemetry.repeatedFindings;
    reviewerMutationCount += telemetry.reviewerMutations;
    unauthorizedActionCount += telemetry.unauthorizedActions;
    recoveryAttempts += telemetry.recoveryAttempts;
    recoverySuccesses += telemetry.recoverySuccesses;
  }
  const outcomes = rows.map(deployOutcome).filter((outcome) => outcome !== void 0);
  const deployAttempts = outcomes.filter(
    (outcome) => DEPLOY_ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES.has(outcome)
  );
  const rollbackAttempts = outcomes.filter(
    (outcome) => ROLLBACK_ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES.has(outcome)
  );
  return {
    window,
    from: from.toISOString(),
    through: through.toISOString(),
    run_count: rows.length,
    metrics: {
      unattended_completion_rate: ratio(
        unattendedCompleted,
        eligibleTerminal.length
      ),
      human_intervention_rate: ratio(
        avoidableHumanInterventions,
        eligibleTerminal.length
      ),
      mean_builder_iterations: mean(
        builderIterations.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0),
        builderIterations.length
      ),
      mean_review_rounds: mean(
        reviewRounds.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0),
        reviewRounds.length
      ),
      repeated_finding_count: repeatedFindingCount,
      reviewer_mutation_count: reviewerMutationCount,
      unauthorized_action_count: unauthorizedActionCount,
      proof_integrity_failure_count: rows.filter(
        (row) => row.state === "delivery_evidence_failed"
      ).length,
      restart_recovery_rate: ratio(recoverySuccesses, recoveryAttempts),
      deploy_success_rate: ratio(
        deployAttempts.filter((outcome) => outcome === "production_healthy").length,
        deployAttempts.length
      ),
      rollback_success_rate: ratio(
        rollbackAttempts.filter(
          (outcome) => outcome === "production_rolled_back"
        ).length,
        rollbackAttempts.length
      ),
      active_freeze_count: activeFreezeCount
    }
  };
}
function attentionRun(row) {
  return {
    run_id: row.autonomy_run_id,
    repo_id: row.repo_id,
    state: row.state,
    updated_at: row.updated_at
  };
}
function createAutonomyReliabilityReport(db2, options = {}) {
  const now = options.now?.() ?? /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
  const throughSeconds = now.getTime() / 1e3;
  const allRows = db2.prepare(
    `SELECT *
       FROM autonomy_run_projection
       ORDER BY updated_at DESC, autonomy_run_id ASC`
  ).all().map((row) => AutonomyRunProjectionRowSchema.parse(row));
  const telemetryByRun = new Map(
    allRows.map((row) => [
      row.autonomy_run_id,
      transitionTelemetry(db2, row)
    ])
  );
  const dailyFrom = new Date(now.getTime() - 864e5);
  const weeklyFrom = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 864e5);
  const dailyRows = allRows.filter(
    (row) => row.updated_at >= dailyFrom.getTime() / 1e3 && row.updated_at <= throughSeconds
  );
  const weeklyRows = allRows.filter(
    (row) => row.updated_at >= weeklyFrom.getTime() / 1e3 && row.updated_at <= throughSeconds
  );
  const freezes = listProductionFreezes(db2, { active_only: true });
  const rowsInState = (state) => allRows.filter((row) => row.state === state).map(attentionRun);
  const rollbackFailed = allRows.filter(
    (row) => deployOutcome(row) === "rollback_failed"
  );
  const adapterUnavailable = allRows.filter(
    (row) => row.state !== "completed" && (telemetryByRun.get(row.autonomy_run_id)?.adapterUnavailable ?? 0) > 0
  );
  return {
    generated_at: now.toISOString(),
    daily: createScorecard(
      dailyRows,
      telemetryByRun,
      "daily",
      dailyFrom,
      now,
      freezes.length
    ),
    weekly: createScorecard(
      weeklyRows,
      telemetryByRun,
      "weekly",
      weeklyFrom,
      now,
      freezes.length
    ),
    notification_outbox: summarizeNotificationOutbox(
      db2,
      throughSeconds
    ),
    attention: {
      awaiting_approval: rowsInState("awaiting_approval"),
      escalated: rowsInState("escalated"),
      blocked_delivery: rowsInState("blocked_delivery"),
      delivery_evidence_failed: rowsInState("delivery_evidence_failed"),
      production_frozen: freezes.map((freeze) => ({
        repo_id: freeze.repo_id,
        source_run_id: freeze.source_run_id,
        reason: freeze.reason,
        frozen_at: freeze.frozen_at
      })),
      rollback_failed: rollbackFailed.map(attentionRun),
      adapter_unavailable: adapterUnavailable.map(attentionRun)
    }
  };
}

var FAILING_CI = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["failure", "cancelled", "timed_out", "startup_failure", "action_required"]);

  const latest = status.ci_runs[0];
  if (latest === void 0) return "none";

}
async function runDailyBrief(deps) {
  const now = deps.now?.() ?? /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
  const date = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const entries = await readRepoRegistry2(deps.registryPath);
  const enabled = entries.filter((entry) => entry.enabled);
  const healths = [];
  for (const entry of enabled) healths.push(await scanRepo(entry));
  const hygieneHistory = readPortfolioKnowledgeHygieneHistory(
    enabled,
    now.getTime()
  );
  const hygieneScans = await scanPortfolioKnowledgeHygiene(enabled, {
    now: () => now,
    history: async (repoId) => hygieneHistory.byRepo.get(repoId) ?? {
      recurring_count: 0
    }
  });
  const portfolio = buildPortfolioReport(
    attachKnowledgeHygieneSummaries(healths, hygieneScans),
    now.toISOString()
  );
  const github = await fetchPortfolioGithubStatus(
    enabled.map((entry) => ({ repo_id: entry.repo_id, path: entry.path })),
    deps.runner
  );
  const { summary: stack, warning: stackWarning } = readStackBoardSummary();
  const { summary: probe, warning: probeWarning } = readProbePortfolioSummary(enabled, now.getTime());
  const { summary: dependencyObligations, warning: dependencyWarning } = await readDependencyObligationSummary(deps.registryPath);
  const { summary: autonomy, warning: autonomyWarning } = readAutonomyReliability(now);
  const warnings = github.filter((g) => g.warning !== void 0).map((g) => `${g.repo_id}: ${g.warning}`);
  if (stackWarning !== void 0) warnings.push(stackWarning);
  if (probeWarning !== void 0) warnings.push(probeWarning);
  if (dependencyWarning !== void 0) warnings.push(dependencyWarning);
  if (autonomyWarning !== void 0) warnings.push(autonomyWarning);
  if (hygieneHistory.warning !== void 0) {
    warnings.push(hygieneHistory.warning);
  }
  warnings.push(...knowledgeHygieneWarnings(portfolio));
  const briefsDir = join3(osstackHome(), "daily-briefs");
  const previous = await readPreviousBrief(briefsDir, date);
  const delta = computeDelta(previous, { portfolio, github });
  const brief = {
    generated_at: now.toISOString(),
    date,
    portfolio,
    github,
    ...stack !== void 0 ? { stack } : {},
    ...probe !== void 0 ? { probe } : {},
    ...dependencyObligations !== void 0 ? { dependency_obligations: dependencyObligations } : {},
    ...autonomy === void 0 ? {} : { autonomy },
    delta,
    // Computed from portfolio + github below, then stored so the dispatchable
    // `run` payloads ride in `result` (= the brief) and are persisted verbatim.
    owner_decisions: [],
    warnings
  };
  brief.owner_decisions = deriveOwnerDecisions(brief);
  const briefPath = join3(briefsDir, `${date}.json`);
  await mkdir(briefsDir, { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(briefPath, `${JSON.stringify(brief, null, 2)}
`, "utf8");
  return { brief, briefPath, result: buildDailyResult(brief, briefPath, enabled), entries: enabled };
}

function computeDelta(previous, current2) {
  if (previous === void 0) {
    return { is_first_run: true, new_prs: [], ci_flips: [], maturity_changes: [] };
  }
  const prevPrNumbers = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const prevCi = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const g of previous.github) {
    prevPrNumbers.set(g.repo_id, new Set(g.pull_requests.map((pr) => pr.number)));
    prevCi.set(g.repo_id, repoCiStatus(g));
  }
  const prevMaturity = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const repo2 of previous.portfolio.repos) prevMaturity.set(repo2.repo_id, repo2.closed_loop_level);
  const new_prs = [];
  const ci_flips = [];
  for (const g of current2.github) {
    const known = prevPrNumbers.get(g.repo_id);
    for (const pr of g.pull_requests) {
      if (known !== void 0 && !known.has(pr.number)) {
        new_prs.push({ repo_id: g.repo_id, number: pr.number, title: pr.title });
      }
    }
    const before = prevCi.get(g.repo_id);
    const after = repoCiStatus(g);
    if (before !== void 0 && before !== after) {
      ci_flips.push({ repo_id: g.repo_id, from: before, to: after });
    }
  }
  const maturity_changes = [];
  for (const repo2 of current2.portfolio.repos) {
    const before = prevMaturity.get(repo2.repo_id);
    if (before !== void 0 && before !== repo2.closed_loop_level) {
      maturity_changes.push({ repo_id: repo2.repo_id, from: before, to: repo2.closed_loop_level });
    }
  }
  return {
    is_first_run: false,
    previous_brief_date: previous.date,
    new_prs,
    ci_flips,
    maturity_changes
  };
}
async function readPreviousBrief(briefsDir, today) {
  let files;
  try {
    files = await readdir(briefsDir);
  } catch {
    return void 0;
  }
  const dated = files.filter((f) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\.json$/.test(f) && f !== `${today}.json`).sort();
  const latest = dated.at(-1);
  if (latest === void 0) return void 0;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile2(join3(briefsDir, latest), "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return void 0;
  }
}
function deriveOwnerDecisions(brief) {
  const decisions = autonomyOwnerDecisions(brief);
  const pathById = new Map(brief.portfolio.repos.map((r) => [r.repo_id, r.path]));
  for (const g of brief.github) {
    const status = repoCiStatus(g);
    if (FAILING_CI.has(status)) {
      const goal = `fix failing CI on ${g.repo_id}: latest run is ${status}`;
      decisions.push({
        command: "repo.fix_ci",
        reason: `${g.repo_id}: latest CI is ${status} \u2014 investigate before shipping`,
        run: {
          command: `osstack start ${shQuote(goal)} --repo-id ${shQuote(g.repo_id)}`,
          argv: ["osstack", "start", goal, "--repo-id", g.repo_id]
        }
      });
    }
  }
  for (const repo2 of brief.portfolio.repos) {
    const reasons = knowledgeHygieneIssues(repo2);
    if (reasons.length === 0) continue;
    const argv = [
      "osstack",
      "neat",
      "check",
      "--repo",
      repo2.path,
      "--all",
      "--fail-on",
      "warning"
    ];
    decisions.push({
      command: "knowledge_hygiene.check",
      reason: `${repo2.repo_id}: knowledge hygiene ${reasons.join(", ")}`,
      run: {
        command: `osstack neat check --repo ${shQuote(repo2.path)} --all --fail-on warning`,
        argv
      }
    });
  }
  for (const g of brief.github) {
    const openPrs = g.pull_requests.filter((pr) => !pr.isDraft);
    if (openPrs.length > 0) {
      decisions.push({
        command: "repo.review_prs",
        reason: `${g.repo_id}: ${openPrs.length} open PR(s) awaiting review`,
        run: reviewPrsRun(g, pathById.get(g.repo_id))
      });
    }
  }
  for (const rec of brief.portfolio.structured_recommendations ?? []) {
    decisions.push({
      command: "portfolio.raise_maturity",
      reason: rec.text,
      run: raiseMaturityRun(rec)
    });
  }
  return decisions.slice(0, 3);
}
function autonomyOwnerDecisions(brief) {
  const attention = brief.autonomy?.attention;
  if (attention === void 0) return [];
  const decisions = [];
  const seenRuns = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  const addRun = (command, reason, runId) => {
    if (seenRuns.has(runId)) return;
    seenRuns.add(runId);
    decisions.push({
      command,
      reason,
      run: {
        command: `osstack autonomy status ${shQuote(runId)} --json`,
        argv: ["osstack", "autonomy", "status", runId, "--json"]
      }
    });
  };
  for (const item of attention.rollback_failed) {
    addRun(
      "autonomy.rollback_failed",
      `${item.repo_id}: rollback failed in ${item.run_id}`,
      item.run_id
    );
  }
  for (const freeze of attention.production_frozen) {
    decisions.push({
      command: "autonomy.production_frozen",
      reason: `${freeze.repo_id}: production frozen \u2014 ${freeze.reason}`,
      run: {
        command: `osstack autonomy freeze request ${shQuote(freeze.repo_id)} --json`,
        argv: [
          "osstack",
          "autonomy",
          "freeze",
          "request",
          freeze.repo_id,
          "--json"
        ]
      }
    });
  }
  for (const item of attention.delivery_evidence_failed) {
    addRun(
      "autonomy.delivery_evidence_failed",
      `${item.repo_id}: proof integrity failed in ${item.run_id}`,
      item.run_id
    );
  }
  for (const item of attention.awaiting_approval) {
    addRun(
      "autonomy.awaiting_approval",
      `${item.repo_id}: signed Owner decision required for ${item.run_id}`,
      item.run_id
    );
  }
  for (const item of attention.escalated) {
    addRun(
      "autonomy.escalated",
      `${item.repo_id}: ${item.run_id} escalated`,
      item.run_id
    );
  }
  for (const item of attention.blocked_delivery) {
    addRun(
      "autonomy.blocked_delivery",
      `${item.repo_id}: ${item.run_id} is waiting for external delivery recovery`,
      item.run_id
    );
  }
  if (attention.adapter_unavailable.length > 0 && decisions.length < 3) {
    decisions.push({
      command: "autonomy.adapter_unavailable",
      reason: `${attention.adapter_unavailable.length} autonomy run(s) require adapter recovery`,
      run: {
        command: "osstack autonomy health --json",
        argv: ["osstack", "autonomy", "health", "--json"]
      }
    });
  }
  return decisions;
}
function knowledgeHygieneWarnings(portfolio) {
  return portfolio.repos.flatMap((repo2) => {
    const issues = knowledgeHygieneIssues(repo2);
    return issues.length === 0 ? [] : [`${repo2.repo_id}: knowledge hygiene ${issues.join(", ")}`];
  });
}
function knowledgeHygieneIssues(repo2) {
  const scanFailed = repo2.findings.some(
    (finding) => finding.startsWith("knowledge hygiene scan failed:")
  );
  const hygiene = repo2.hygiene;
  if (hygiene === void 0) return scanFailed ? ["scan failed"] : [];
  return [
    ...!hygiene.complete ? ["scan incomplete"] : [],
    ...hygiene.health_claim === "assessment_incomplete" ? ["assessment incomplete"] : [],
    ...hygiene.error_count > 0 ? [`${hygiene.error_count} open error(s)`] : [],
    ...hygiene.warning_count > 0 && hygiene.recurring_count > 0 ? [`${hygiene.recurring_count} recurring warning(s)`] : []
  ];
}
function reviewPrsRun(g, repoPath) {
  if (g.remote !== void 0) {
    return {
      command: `gh pr list --repo ${shQuote(g.remote)} --state open`,
      argv: ["gh", "pr", "list", "--repo", g.remote, "--state", "open"]
    };
  }
  const target = repoPath ?? g.repo_id;
  return { command: shQuote(target), argv: [target] };
}
function raiseMaturityRun(rec) {
  if (rec.kind === "add_loop_doc" && rec.repo_id !== void 0) {
    const repoId = rec.repo_id;
    return {
      command: `osstack supervise init-loop-doc --repo ${shQuote(repoId)}`,
      argv: ["osstack", "supervise", "init-loop-doc", "--repo", repoId]
    };
  }
  return { command: "osstack supervise report", argv: ["osstack", "supervise", "report"] };
}
function buildDailyResult(brief, briefPath, entries) {
  const pathById = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.repo_id, e.path]));
  const evidence_refs = [
    { kind: "report", ref: briefPath }
  ];
  for (const g of brief.github) {
    evidence_refs.push({
      kind: "external",
      ref: g.remote !== void 0 ? `https://github.com/${g.remote}` : pathById.get(g.repo_id) ?? g.repo_id
    });
  }
  const next_actions = (brief.owner_decisions ?? []).map((d) => ({ command: d.command, reason: d.reason }));
  const result = {
    protocol_version: PROTOCOL_VERSION2,
    status: "ok",
    result: brief,
    evidence_refs,
    ...next_actions.length > 0 ? { next_actions } : {}
  };
  return result;
}

// packages/cli/src/daemon.ts
import { resolveRepositoryScope as resolveRepositoryScope3 } from "@osstack/core";

// packages/cli/src/version.ts
import { readFileSync as readFileSync2 } from "node:fs";
import { dirname as dirname3, join as join4 } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath as fileURLToPath2 } from "node:url";
function readPackageVersion() {
  const injected = process.env.OSSTACK_VERSION;
  if (typeof injected === "string" && injected.length > 0) return injected;
  try {
    const here = dirname3(fileURLToPath2(import.meta.url));
    const raw = readFileSync2(join4(here, "..", "package.json"), "utf8");
    const pkg = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (typeof pkg.version === "string" && pkg.version.length > 0) return pkg.version;
  } catch {
  }
  return "0.0.0";
}
var VERSION = readPackageVersion();
var PROTOCOL_VERSION3 = 1;
function compareSemver(a, b) {
  const pa = a.replace(/^v/, "").split("-")[0].split(".").map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const pb = b.replace(/^v/, "").split("-")[0].split(".").map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const x = pa[i] ?? 0;
    const y = pb[i] ?? 0;
    if (x < y) return -1;
    if (x > y) return 1;
  }
  return 0;
}

// packages/cli/src/update-check.ts
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync as existsSync3, mkdirSync as mkdirSync2, readFileSync as readFileSync3, writeFileSync as writeFileSync2 } from "node:fs";
import { dirname as dirname4 } from "node:path";
var UPDATE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3;
var REGISTRY_TIMEOUT_MS = 2e4;
function isUpdateCacheFresh(cache, nowSeconds = Date.now() / 1e3, currentVersion = VERSION) {
  return cache !== null && cache.current === currentVersion && nowSeconds - cache.checked_at < UPDATE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS / 1e3;
}
function updateCheckEnabled() {
  return process.env["OSSTACK_UPDATE_CHECK"] !== "0";
}
function readUpdateCache() {
  try {
    const raw = readFileSync3(updateCheckCachePath(), "utf8");
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function writeUpdateCache(cache) {
  try {
    const path = updateCheckCachePath();
    if (!existsSync3(dirname4(path))) mkdirSync2(dirname4(path), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync2(path, `${JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2)}
`);
  } catch {
  }
}
function fetchRegistryVersion(pkg, tag = "latest") {
  return new Promise((resolve4) => {
    const p = spawn("npm", ["view", `${pkg}@${tag}`, "version"], {
      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"]
    });
    let out = "";
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      p.kill("SIGTERM");
      resolve4(null);
    }, REGISTRY_TIMEOUT_MS);
    p.stdout.on("data", (c) => {
      out += String(c);
    });
    p.on("error", () => {
      clearTimeout(t);
      resolve4(null);
    });
    p.on("exit", (code) => {
      clearTimeout(t);
      const v = out.trim().split("\n").pop()?.trim() ?? "";
      resolve4(code === 0 && /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(v) ? v : null);
    });
  });
}
async function runUpdateCheck() {
  const latest = await fetchRegistryVersion("@osstack/cli");
  const cache = {
    checked_at: Date.now() / 1e3,
    current: VERSION,
    latest,
    update_available: latest !== null && compareSemver(latest, VERSION) > 0,
    ...latest === null ? { reason: "registry_unreachable_or_unpublished" } : {}
  };
  writeUpdateCache(cache);
  return cache;
}
function startUpdateChecker() {
  if (!updateCheckEnabled()) return () => {
  };
  const probeIfStale = () => {
    const cache = readUpdateCache();
    if (!isUpdateCacheFresh(cache)) void runUpdateCheck();
  };
  const initial = setTimeout(probeIfStale, 6e4);
  initial.unref();
  const interval = setInterval(probeIfStale, UPDATE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);
  interval.unref();
  return () => {
    clearTimeout(initial);
    clearInterval(interval);
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/daemon-lock.ts
import { existsSync as existsSync4, mkdirSync as mkdirSync3, readFileSync as readFileSync4, unlinkSync, writeFileSync as writeFileSync3 } from "node:fs";
import { dirname as dirname5 } from "node:path";
var DaemonAlreadyRunningError = class extends Error {
  pid;
  constructor(pid, pidFile) {
    super(`osstack daemon already running (pid ${pid}, pidfile ${pidFile}). Stop it first or remove the stale pidfile.`);
    this.name = "DaemonAlreadyRunningError";
    this.pid = pid;
  }
};
function processAlive(pid) {
  try {
    process.kill(pid, 0);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return err.code === "EPERM";
  }
}
function acquireDaemonLock(pidFile) {
  if (existsSync4(pidFile)) {
    const pid = Number.parseInt(readFileSync4(pidFile, "utf8").trim(), 10);
    if (Number.isFinite(pid) && pid > 0 && processAlive(pid)) {
      throw new DaemonAlreadyRunningError(pid, pidFile);
    }
  }
  if (!existsSync4(dirname5(pidFile))) mkdirSync3(dirname5(pidFile), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync3(pidFile, `${process.pid}
`);
  return () => {
    try {
      const pid = Number.parseInt(readFileSync4(pidFile, "utf8").trim(), 10);
      if (pid === process.pid) unlinkSync(pidFile);
    } catch {
    }
  };
}

import {
