var STANDARD_RETENTION_STATES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["cancelled", "completed"]);
var EXTENDED_RETENTION_STATES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
  "blocked",
  "delivery_evidence_failed",
  "escalated"
]);
var ClockJumpDetector = class {
  constructor(thresholdMs = 12e4) {
    this.thresholdMs = thresholdMs;
    if (!Number.isFinite(thresholdMs) || thresholdMs < 1) {
      throw new Error("clock jump threshold must be positive");
    }
  }
  thresholdMs;
  previousWallMs;
  previousMonotonicMs;
  observe(wallMs, monotonicMs) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(wallMs) || !Number.isFinite(monotonicMs)) {
      throw new Error("clock observations must be finite");
    }
    const previousWall = this.previousWallMs;
    const previousMonotonic = this.previousMonotonicMs;
    this.previousWallMs = wallMs;
    this.previousMonotonicMs = monotonicMs;
    if (previousWall === void 0 || previousMonotonic === void 0) {
      return {
        jump_detected: false,
        drift_ms: 0,
        wall_elapsed_ms: 0,
        monotonic_elapsed_ms: 0
      };
    }
    const wallElapsed = wallMs - previousWall;
    const monotonicElapsed = monotonicMs - previousMonotonic;
    const drift = wallElapsed - monotonicElapsed;
    return {
      jump_detected: Math.abs(drift) >= this.thresholdMs,
      drift_ms: drift,
      wall_elapsed_ms: wallElapsed,
      monotonic_elapsed_ms: monotonicElapsed
    };
  }
};
function positiveInteger(value, fallback, label) {
  const resolved = value ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be a positive integer`);
  }
  return resolved;
}
function retentionConfig(options) {
  return {
    now: options.now?.() ?? Date.now() / 1e3,
    maxLogBytes: positiveInteger(
      options.max_log_bytes,
      25 * 1024 ** 2,
      "maximum log bytes"
    ),
    maxLogFiles: positiveInteger(
      options.max_log_files,
      5,
      "maximum rotated log files"
    ),
    proofRetentionDays: positiveInteger(
      options.proof_retention_days,
      30,
      "proof retention days"
    ),
    proofMinKeep: positiveInteger(
      options.proof_min_keep,
      100,
      "minimum proofs to keep"
    )
  };
}
async function regularFiles(directory) {
  let names;
  try {
    names = await readdir2(directory);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
  const files = [];
  for (const name of names.sort()) {
    if (!name.endsWith(".log")) continue;
    const path = join5(directory, name);
    const metadata = await lstat(path);
    if (metadata.isFile() && !metadata.isSymbolicLink()) files.push(path);
  }
  return files;
}
async function proofDirectories(directory) {
  let names;
  try {
    names = await readdir2(directory);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
  const directories = [];
  for (const name of names.sort()) {
    if (!/^run_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(name)) continue;
    const path = join5(directory, name);
    const metadata = await lstat(path);
    if (!metadata.isDirectory() || metadata.isSymbolicLink()) continue;
    directories.push({
      name,
      path,
      modified_at: metadata.mtimeMs / 1e3
    });
  }
  return directories.sort(
    (left, right) => right.modified_at - left.modified_at || left.name.localeCompare(right.name)
  );
}
function terminalRunStates(db2) {
  const rows = db2.prepare(
    `SELECT autonomy_run_id, state
       FROM autonomy_run_projection`
  ).all();
  return new Map(
    rows.filter(
      (row) => STANDARD_RETENTION_STATES.has(row.state) || EXTENDED_RETENTION_STATES.has(row.state)
    ).map((row) => [row.autonomy_run_id, row.state])
  );
}
async function retentionSnapshot(options) {
  const config = retentionConfig(options);
  const logs = await Promise.all(
    (await regularFiles(join5(options.home, "logs"))).map(async (path) => ({
      path,
      size: (await stat(path)).size
    }))
  );
  const proofDirs = await proofDirectories(join5(options.home, "autonomy"));
  const terminal = terminalRunStates(options.db);
  const cutoff = config.now - config.proofRetentionDays * 86400;
  const extendedCutoff = config.now - config.proofRetentionDays * 3 * 86400;
  const minimumKept = new Set(
    proofDirs.slice(0, config.proofMinKeep).map((entry) => entry.name)
  );
  const expiredProofs = proofDirs.filter((entry) => !minimumKept.has(entry.name)).filter((entry) => {
    const state = terminal.get(entry.name);
    if (state === void 0) return false;
    return entry.modified_at < (EXTENDED_RETENTION_STATES.has(state) ? extendedCutoff : cutoff);
  }).map(({ name, path }) => ({ name, path }));
  return { config, logs, expiredProofs };
}
async function inspectAutonomyRetention(options) {
  const { config, logs, expiredProofs } = await retentionSnapshot(options);
  return {
    checked_at: config.now,
    max_log_bytes: config.maxLogBytes,
    max_log_files: config.maxLogFiles,
    proof_retention_days: config.proofRetentionDays,
    proof_min_keep: config.proofMinKeep,
    largest_log_bytes: Math.max(0, ...logs.map((entry) => entry.size)),
    oversized_log_count: logs.filter(
      (entry) => entry.size > config.maxLogBytes
    ).length,
    expired_terminal_proof_count: expiredProofs.length,
    rotated_logs: [],
    removed_proof_dirs: []
  };
}
async function rotateLog(path, maxFiles) {
  await rm(`${path}.${maxFiles}`, { force: true });
  for (let index = maxFiles - 1; index >= 1; index -= 1) {
    try {
      await rename(`${path}.${index}`, `${path}.${index + 1}`);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof Error) || !("code" in error) || error.code !== "ENOENT") {
        throw error;
      }
    }
  }
  await copyFile(path, `${path}.1`);
  await truncate(path, 0);
}
async function enforceAutonomyRetention(options) {
  await mkdir2(join5(options.home, "logs"), { recursive: true });
  await mkdir2(join5(options.home, "autonomy"), { recursive: true });
  const snapshot2 = await retentionSnapshot(options);
  const rotatedLogs = [];
  for (const log of snapshot2.logs) {
    if (log.size <= snapshot2.config.maxLogBytes) continue;
    await rotateLog(log.path, snapshot2.config.maxLogFiles);
    rotatedLogs.push(log.path);
  }
  const removedProofDirs = [];
  for (const proof of snapshot2.expiredProofs) {
    await rm(proof.path, { recursive: true, force: false });
    removedProofDirs.push(proof.path);
  }
  const inspected = await inspectAutonomyRetention(options);
  return {
    ...inspected,
    rotated_logs: rotatedLogs,
    removed_proof_dirs: removedProofDirs
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/autonomy-health.ts
var execFileAsync = promisify2(execFile2);
var AUTONOMY_MIN_WRITE_FREE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 ** 3;
var AUTONOMY_DRAIN_FREE_BYTES = 2 * 1024 ** 3;
function check(id, status, scope, detail) {
  return { id, status, scope, detail };
}
function defaultDiskFreeBytes(dbPath) {
  const stats = statfsSync(dirname6(dbPath));
  return Number(stats.bsize) * Number(stats.bavail);
}
async function defaultAdapterProbe() {
  const { defaultAdapters: defaultAdapters2 } = await import("@osstack/agents");
  return await Promise.all(defaultAdapters2().map(async (adapter) => ({
    id: adapter.id,
    available: (await adapter.isAvailable()).available,
    capability_kinds: adapter.capabilities().map(
      (capability) => capability.kind
    )
  })));
}
async function commandProbe(command, args) {
  try {
    await execFileAsync(command, args, {
      timeout: 5e3,
      maxBuffer: 64 * 1024
    });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
async function defaultPowerAssertionProbe() {
  if (process.platform !== "darwin") return false;
  try {
    const { stdout } = await execFileAsync("pmset", ["-g", "assertions"], {
      timeout: 5e3,
      maxBuffer: 64 * 1024
    });
    return /Prevent(?:UserIdle)?SystemSleep\s+1/.test(stdout);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
async function defaultLaunchdProbe() {
  if (process.platform !== "darwin") return { loaded: false, output: "" };
  try {
    const { stdout, stderr } = await execFileAsync(
      "launchctl",
      [
        "print",
        `gui/${process.getuid?.() ?? 501}/ai.osstack.daemon`
      ],
      { timeout: 5e3, maxBuffer: 256 * 1024 }
    );
    return { loaded: true, output: `${stdout}${stderr}` };
  } catch {
    return { loaded: false, output: "" };
  }
}
function positiveEnvironmentInteger(name, fallback) {
  const raw = process.env[name];
  if (raw === void 0) return fallback;
  if (!/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(raw)) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive integer`);
  }
  return Number(raw);
}
function powerPolicyRecordMatches(home, mode) {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(
      readFileSync5(join6(home, "host", "power-policy.json"), "utf8")
    );
    if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {
      return false;
    }
    const record = value;
    return record["schema_version"] === 1 && record["mode"] === mode && record["machine_settings_changed"] === false && typeof record["current"] === "string" && typeof record["rollback"] === "string";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
function repoRegistryCheck(path) {
  if (!existsSync5(path)) {
    return check(
      "repo_registry",
      "warn",
      "none",
      "repository registry is not configured"
    );
  }
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(readFileSync5(path, "utf-8"));
    if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
      return check(
        "repo_registry",
        "blocked",
        "writes",
        "repository registry is not a JSON array"
      );
    }
    return check(
      "repo_registry",
      "ok",
      "none",
      `${value.length} registered repositories`
    );
  } catch {
    return check(
      "repo_registry",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      "repository registry cannot be parsed"
    );
  }
}
function repoLocksCheck(lockDirectory) {
  if (!existsSync5(lockDirectory)) {
    return check("repo_locks", "ok", "none", "no repository write locks");
  }
  let locks = 0;
  try {
    for (const name of readdirSync(lockDirectory)) {
      if (name.includes(".lock.stale-")) {
        throw new Error("incomplete stale lock recovery");
      }
      if (!name.endsWith(".lock")) continue;
      locks += 1;
      const path = join6(lockDirectory, name);
      const stats = lstatSync(path);
      if (!stats.isFile() || stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
        throw new Error("lock is not a regular file");
      }
      const value = JSON.parse(readFileSync5(path, "utf-8"));
      if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value) || typeof value["pid"] !== "number" || typeof value["autonomy_run_id"] !== "string" || typeof value["repo_id"] !== "string" || typeof value["acquired_at"] !== "string") {
        throw new Error("lock schema is invalid");
      }
    }
  } catch {
    return check(
      "repo_locks",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      "repository lock inventory contains an invalid lock"
    );
  }
  return check(
    "repo_locks",
    "ok",
    "none",
    `${locks} active repository write locks`
  );
}
function socketCheck(home, socketPath) {
  if (!existsSync5(socketPath)) {
    return check(
      "daemon_socket",
      "warn",
      "none",
      "daemon socket is not present"
    );
  }
  try {
    if (!lstatSync(socketPath).isSocket()) {
      return check(
        "daemon_socket",
        "blocked",
        "daemon",
        "daemon socket path is not a Unix socket"
      );
    }
    const pid = Number(
      readFileSync5(join6(home, "daemon.pid"), "utf-8").trim()
    );
    if (!Number.isInteger(pid) || pid < 1) {
      return check(
        "daemon_socket",
        "warn",
        "none",
        "daemon socket exists without a valid pidfile"
      );
    }
    return check(
      "daemon_socket",
      "ok",
      "none",
      "daemon socket and pidfile are present"
    );
  } catch {
    return check(
      "daemon_socket",
      "warn",
      "none",
      "daemon socket ownership could not be confirmed"
    );
  }
}
function nextActions(checks, drainRequired, notificationOutbox) {
  const actions = [];
  const blocked = new Set(
    checks.filter((candidate) => candidate.status === "blocked").map((candidate) => candidate.id)
  );
  if (drainRequired) {
    actions.push("Drain the daemon and free disk space immediately");
  } else if (blocked.has("disk_free")) {
    actions.push("Free at least 5 GiB before starting another write run");
  }
  if (blocked.has("repo_locks")) {
    actions.push("Inspect corrupt repository locks before resuming writes");
  }
  if (blocked.has("builder_agents")) {
    actions.push("Restore at least one available code-writing Builder");
  }
  if (blocked.has("kimi_code")) {
    actions.push("Restore Kimi Code availability before merge");
  }
  if (blocked.has("github_auth")) {
    actions.push("Refresh GitHub CLI authentication");
  }
  if (blocked.has("github_network")) {
    actions.push("Wait for GitHub network recovery; local work may continue");
  }
  if (notificationOutbox.dead_letter_count > 0) {
    actions.push(
      "Inspect and acknowledge dead-letter Owner notifications"
    );
  } else if (notificationOutbox.pending_count > 0) {
    actions.push("Restore the Owner notification relay and drain the outbox");
  }
  if (blocked.has("power_policy")) {
    actions.push(
      "Restore the selected process-bound power assertion before soak"
    );
  }
  if (blocked.has("launchd_service")) {
    actions.push("Restore ai.osstack.daemon and capture launchctl print evidence");
  }
  if (blocked.has("log_retention")) {
    actions.push("Repair autonomy log/proof retention before new writes");
  }
  return actions;
}
async function runAutonomyHealth(deps) {
  const checks = [];
  try {
    deps.db.prepare("SELECT 1 AS ok").get();
    checks.push(check("state_db", "ok", "none", "state database is readable"));
  } catch {
    checks.push(check(
      "state_db",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      "state database query failed"
    ));
  }
  checks.push(socketCheck(deps.home, deps.socket_path));
  let diskFreeBytes = 0;
  let diskProbeFailed = false;
  try {
    diskFreeBytes = (deps.disk_free_bytes ?? (() => defaultDiskFreeBytes(deps.db_path)))();
    if (!Number.isFinite(diskFreeBytes) || diskFreeBytes < 0) {
      throw new Error("disk-free probe returned an invalid value");
    }
  } catch {
    diskProbeFailed = true;
    diskFreeBytes = 0;
  }
  const drainRequired = diskFreeBytes < AUTONOMY_DRAIN_FREE_BYTES;
  checks.push(
    diskProbeFailed ? check(
      "disk_free",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      "disk-free probe failed; daemon drain required"
    ) : diskFreeBytes < AUTONOMY_MIN_WRITE_FREE_BYTES ? check(
      "disk_free",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      drainRequired ? `${diskFreeBytes} bytes free; daemon drain required below 2 GiB` : `${diskFreeBytes} bytes free; writes require at least 5 GiB`
    ) : check(
      "disk_free",
      "ok",
      "none",
      `${diskFreeBytes} bytes free`
    )
  );
  checks.push(repoRegistryCheck(
    deps.repo_registry_path ?? join6(deps.home, "repos.json")
  ));
  checks.push(repoLocksCheck(join6(deps.home, "locks", "repos")));
  let adapters = [];
  try {
    adapters = await (deps.probe_adapters ?? defaultAdapterProbe)();
  } catch {
    adapters = [];
  }
  const kimiAvailable = adapters.some(
    (adapter) => adapter.id === "kimi-code" && adapter.available
  );
  checks.push(
    kimiAvailable ? check("kimi_code", "ok", "none", "Kimi Code is available") : check(
      "kimi_code",
      "blocked",
      "external",
      "Kimi Code is unavailable; merge remains blocked"
    )
  );
  const builders = adapters.filter(
    (adapter) => adapter.available && adapter.capability_kinds.includes("code.write")
  );
  checks.push(
    builders.length > 0 ? check(
      "builder_agents",
      "ok",
      "none",
      `${builders.length} code-writing builders available`
    ) : check(
      "builder_agents",
      "blocked",
      "writes",
      "no code-writing Builder is available"
    )
  );
  const githubAuth = await (deps.probe_github_auth ?? (() => commandProbe("gh", ["auth", "status", "-h", "github.com"])))();
  checks.push(
    githubAuth ? check("github_auth", "ok", "none", "GitHub authentication is valid") : check(
      "github_auth",
      "blocked",
      "external",
      "GitHub authentication probe failed"
    )
  );
  const githubNetwork = await (deps.probe_github_network ?? (() => commandProbe("gh", ["api", "rate_limit", "--silent"])))();
  checks.push(
    githubNetwork ? check("github_network", "ok", "none", "GitHub network is reachable") : check(
      "github_network",
      "blocked",
      "external",
      "GitHub network probe failed"
    )
  );
  const activeFreezes = listProductionFreezes2(
    deps.db,
    { active_only: true }
  ).map((freeze) => ({
    repo_id: freeze.repo_id,
    reason: freeze.reason,
    source_run_id: freeze.source_run_id,
    frozen_at: freeze.frozen_at
  }));
  checks.push(check(
    "deploy_freezes",
    activeFreezes.length === 0 ? "ok" : "warn",
    activeFreezes.length === 0 ? "none" : "external",
    activeFreezes.length === 0 ? "no active production freezes" : `${activeFreezes.length} production freezes require review`
  ));
  const resumableRuns = listResumableAutonomyRuns(deps.db).map((row) => ({
    autonomy_run_id: row.autonomy_run_id,
    repo_id: row.repo_id,
    state: row.state
  }));
  checks.push(check(
    "resumable_runs",
    resumableRuns.length === 0 ? "ok" : "warn",
    "none",
    `${resumableRuns.length} resumable autonomy runs`
  ));
  const proofFailures = listAutonomyRuns(deps.db, { limit: 1e3 }).filter(
    (row) => row.state === "delivery_evidence_failed" || row.state === "escalated"
  ).map((row) => ({
    autonomy_run_id: row.autonomy_run_id,
    repo_id: row.repo_id,
    state: row.state
  }));
  checks.push(check(
    "proof_failures",
    proofFailures.length === 0 ? "ok" : "warn",
    "none",
    `${proofFailures.length} proof or escalated run failures`
  ));
  const notificationOutbox = summarizeNotificationOutbox2(
    deps.db,
    deps.now?.() ?? Date.now() / 1e3
  );
  checks.push(check(
    "notification_outbox",
    notificationOutbox.pending_count === 0 && notificationOutbox.dead_letter_count === 0 ? "ok" : "warn",
    notificationOutbox.dead_letter_count > 0 ? "external" : "none",
    notificationOutbox.pending_count === 0 && notificationOutbox.dead_letter_count === 0 ? "notification outbox is drained" : `${notificationOutbox.pending_count} pending and ${notificationOutbox.dead_letter_count} dead-letter Owner notifications; oldest attention age ${notificationOutbox.oldest_attention_age_seconds}s`
  ));
  const powerPolicy = deps.power_policy ?? (process.env["OSSTACK_POWER_POLICY"] === "process_assertion" ? "process_assertion" : "none");
  const powerAssertionActive = powerPolicy === "process_assertion" ? await (deps.probe_power_assertion ?? defaultPowerAssertionProbe)() : false;
  const powerPolicyRecorded = deps.power_policy_record_valid ?? powerPolicyRecordMatches(deps.home, powerPolicy);
  checks.push(check(
    "power_policy",
    powerPolicy === "none" ? "warn" : powerAssertionActive && powerPolicyRecorded ? "ok" : "blocked",
    powerPolicy === "process_assertion" && (!powerAssertionActive || !powerPolicyRecorded) ? "daemon" : "none",
    powerPolicy === "none" ? "no power assertion selected; explicit Owner selection is required before soak" : powerAssertionActive && powerPolicyRecorded ? "process-bound system-sleep assertion is active and recorded with rollback" : powerAssertionActive ? "power assertion is active but the selection/rollback record is missing" : "selected process-bound power assertion is not active"
  ));
  const clockMonitorActive = deps.clock_monitor ?? process.env["OSSTACK_CLOCK_MONITOR"] === "monotonic-v1";
  checks.push(check(
    "clock_monitor",
    clockMonitorActive ? "ok" : "warn",
    "none",
    clockMonitorActive ? "monotonic-v1 clock jump detection is active" : "clock jump detector is not declared active"
  ));
  let retention = null;
  let retentionError;
  try {
    retention = await (deps.inspect_retention ?? (() => inspectAutonomyRetention({
      home: deps.home,
      db: deps.db,
      ...deps.now === void 0 ? {} : { now: deps.now },
      max_log_bytes: positiveEnvironmentInteger(
        "OSSTACK_LOG_MAX_BYTES",
        25 * 1024 ** 2
      ),
      max_log_files: positiveEnvironmentInteger(
        "OSSTACK_LOG_MAX_FILES",
        5
      ),
      proof_retention_days: positiveEnvironmentInteger(
        "OSSTACK_PROOF_RETENTION_DAYS",
        30
      ),
      proof_min_keep: positiveEnvironmentInteger(
        "OSSTACK_PROOF_MIN_KEEP",
        100
      )
    })))();
  } catch (error) {
    retentionError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  }
  const retentionHealthy = retention !== null && retention.oversized_log_count === 0 && retention.expired_terminal_proof_count === 0;
  checks.push(check(
    "log_retention",
    retention === null ? "blocked" : retentionHealthy ? "ok" : "warn",
    retention === null ? "daemon" : "none",
    retention === null ? `retention inspection failed: ${retentionError ?? "unknown error"}` : `${retention.oversized_log_count} oversized logs and ${retention.expired_terminal_proof_count} expired terminal proof directories`
  ));
  const launchdRequired = deps.require_launchd ?? false;
  const launchd = await (deps.probe_launchd ?? defaultLaunchdProbe)();
  const launchdDigest = launchd.loaded && launchd.output.length > 0 ? createHash3("sha256").update(launchd.output).digest("hex") : null;
  checks.push(check(
    "launchd_service",
    launchd.loaded ? "ok" : launchdRequired ? "blocked" : "warn",
    !launchd.loaded && launchdRequired ? "daemon" : "none",
    launchd.loaded ? `launchctl print evidence sha256=${launchdDigest}` : launchdRequired ? "required ai.osstack.daemon launchd service is not loaded" : "launchd service is not required in this execution mode"
  ));
  const externalBlockers = checks.filter(
    (candidate) => candidate.status === "blocked" && candidate.scope === "external"
  ).map((candidate) => candidate.id);
  const writeBlockers = checks.filter(
    (candidate) => candidate.status === "blocked" && (candidate.scope === "writes" || candidate.scope === "daemon")
  ).map((candidate) => candidate.id);
  const canRunLocal = writeBlockers.length === 0;
  const canPushDeploy = canRunLocal && !externalBlockers.includes("github_auth") && !externalBlockers.includes("github_network");
  const canMerge = canPushDeploy && !externalBlockers.includes("kimi_code");
  return {
    ok: checks.every((candidate) => candidate.status !== "blocked"),
    can_run_local: canRunLocal,
    can_merge: canMerge,
    can_push_deploy: canPushDeploy,
    drain_required: drainRequired,
    disk_free_bytes: diskFreeBytes,
    checks,
    active_freezes: activeFreezes,
    resumable_runs: resumableRuns,
    proof_failures: proofFailures,
    notification_outbox: notificationOutbox,
    host_evidence: {
      power_policy: {
        mode: powerPolicy,
        assertion_active: powerAssertionActive,
        recorded: powerPolicyRecorded
      },
      clock_monitor: {
        mode: clockMonitorActive ? "monotonic-v1" : "none",
        active: clockMonitorActive
      },
      retention,
      launchd: {
        required: launchdRequired,
        loaded: launchd.loaded,
        print_sha256: launchdDigest
      }
    },
    external_blockers: externalBlockers,
    write_blockers: writeBlockers,
    next_actions: nextActions(
      checks,
      drainRequired,
      notificationOutbox
    )
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/autonomy-notification.ts
import {
  listDueNotifications,
  notificationPayload,
  recordNotificationAttempt
} from "@osstack/core";
var NotificationInvalidResponseError = class extends Error {
  constructor(message = "notification transport returned an invalid response") {
    super(message);
    this.name = "NotificationInvalidResponseError";
  }
};
var NotificationTimeoutError = class extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("notification delivery timed out");
    this.name = "NotificationTimeoutError";
  }
};
function positiveInteger2(value, fallback, label) {
  const resolved = value ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be a positive integer`);
  }
  return resolved;
}
function failureCode(error) {
  if (error instanceof NotificationTimeoutError) return "timeout";
  if (error instanceof NotificationInvalidResponseError) {
    return "invalid_response";
  }
  return "transport_error";
}
function backoffMs(row, baseBackoffMs, maxBackoffMs) {
  return Math.min(
    maxBackoffMs,
    baseBackoffMs * 2 ** Math.min(row.attempt_count, 20)
  );
}
async function sendWithTimeout(sender, row, timeoutMs) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  let timer;
  try {
    await Promise.race([
      sender.send(
        {
          notification_id: row.notification_id,
          idempotency_key: row.notification_id,
          payload: notificationPayload(row)
        },
        controller.signal
      ),
      new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
          controller.abort();
          reject(new NotificationTimeoutError());
        }, timeoutMs);
      })
    ]);
  } finally {
    if (timer !== void 0) clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}
async function drainAutonomyNotifications(options) {
  const timeoutMs = positiveInteger2(
    options.timeout_ms,
    1e4,
    "notification timeout"
  );
  const baseBackoffMs = positiveInteger2(
    options.base_backoff_ms,
    3e4,
    "notification base backoff"
  );
  const maxBackoffMs = positiveInteger2(
    options.max_backoff_ms,
    36e5,
    "notification max backoff"
  );
  const maxAttempts = positiveInteger2(
    options.max_attempts,
    5,
    "notification max attempts"
  );
  const limit = positiveInteger2(options.limit, 50, "notification batch limit");
  if (maxBackoffMs < baseBackoffMs) {
    throw new Error(
      "notification max backoff must be at least the base backoff"
    );
  }
  if (maxAttempts > 20) {
    throw new Error("notification max attempts cannot exceed 20");
  }
  if (limit > 500) {
    throw new Error("notification batch limit cannot exceed 500");
  }
  const now = options.now ?? (() => Date.now() / 1e3);
  const rows = listDueNotifications(options.db, now(), limit);
  let delivered = 0;
  let retried = 0;
  let deadLettered = 0;
  for (const row of rows) {
    try {
      await sendWithTimeout(options.sender, row, timeoutMs);
      recordNotificationAttempt(options.db, options.db_path, {
        notification_id: row.notification_id,
        expected_attempt_count: row.attempt_count,
        outcome: "delivered",
        attempted_at: now()
      });
      delivered += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      const attemptedAt = now();
      const next = recordNotificationAttempt(options.db, options.db_path, {
        notification_id: row.notification_id,
        expected_attempt_count: row.attempt_count,
        outcome: "failed",
        error_code: failureCode(error),
        attempted_at: attemptedAt,
        max_attempts: maxAttempts,
        next_attempt_at: attemptedAt + backoffMs(row, baseBackoffMs, maxBackoffMs) / 1e3
      });
      if (next.status === "dead_letter") deadLettered += 1;
      else retried += 1;
    }
  }
  return {
    selected: rows.length,
    delivered,
    retried,
    dead_lettered: deadLettered
  };
}

// packages/cli/src/dashboard.ts
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { readFileSync as readFileSync6, readdirSync as readdirSync2, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname as dirname7, join as join7 } from "node:path";
import {
  countEvents,
  countPendingTasks,
  getBoard as getBoard2,
  listAgentScorecards,
  listPitfallLessons,
  listProductionFreezes as listProductionFreezes3
} from "@osstack/core";
function dashboardUrl(opts) {
  const host = opts.urlHost ?? opts.host;
  return `http://${host}:${opts.port}/dashboard`;
}
function dashboardUrlFromEnv() {
  const defaultPort = process.env["VITEST"] === "true" ? "0" : "8799";
  return {
    host: process.env["OSSTACK_DASHBOARD_HOST"] ?? "127.0.0.1",
    urlHost: process.env["OSSTACK_DASHBOARD_URL_HOST"] ?? "localhost",
    port: Number(process.env["OSSTACK_DASHBOARD_PORT"] ?? defaultPort)
  };
}
function createDashboardSnapshot(opts) {
  const generatedAt = opts.now ? opts.now() : Date.now() / 1e3;
  const runCounts = opts.db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      COUNT(*) AS total_runs,
      SUM(CASE WHEN outcome = 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS completed_runs,
      SUM(CASE WHEN outcome = 'failed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failed_runs,
      SUM(CASE WHEN outcome = 'cancelled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS cancelled_runs,
      SUM(tokens_in) AS tokens_in,
      SUM(tokens_out) AS tokens_out,
      AVG(duration_ms) AS avg_duration_ms
    FROM runs
  `).get();
  const totalEvents = countEvents(opts.db);
  const queuedTasks = countPendingTasks(opts.db);
  const boardOptions = opts.now ? { includeArchived: false, now: opts.now } : { includeArchived: false };
  const boardView = getBoard2(opts.kanban, boardOptions);
  const boardColumns = boardView.changed ? boardView.columns : [];
  const byStatus = boardView.changed ? boardView.stats.by_status : {};
  const boardTotal = boardView.changed ? boardView.stats.total : 0;
  const doneCards = byStatus["done"] ?? 0;
  const progressBase = boardTotal > 0 ? boardTotal : runCounts.total_runs;
  const progressDone = boardTotal > 0 ? doneCards : runCounts.completed_runs ?? 0;
  const progressPercent = progressBase > 0 ? Math.round(progressDone / progressBase * 100) : 0;
  const completed = runCounts.completed_runs ?? 0;
  const failed = runCounts.failed_runs ?? 0;
  const cancelled = runCounts.cancelled_runs ?? 0;
  const terminalRuns = completed + failed + cancelled;
  const successRate = terminalRuns > 0 ? completed / terminalRuns : 1;
  const blockedCards = byStatus["blocked"] ?? 0;
  const grade = successRate >= 0.9 && blockedCards === 0 ? "A" : successRate >= 0.5 ? "B" : "C";
  const recentRuns = opts.db.prepare(`
    SELECT r.run_id, r.task_id, t.title, r.agent_id, r.capability_id, r.phase,
           r.outcome, r.started_at, r.duration_ms
    FROM runs r JOIN tasks t ON t.task_id = r.task_id
    ORDER BY r.started_at DESC
    LIMIT 8
  `).all();
  const workspacePath = opts.workspacePath ?? dirnameOfDb(opts.dbPath);
  const reportDir = join7(workspacePath, "reports");
  const releaseDir = join7(workspacePath, "release");
  const reportsCount = countMarkdownFiles(reportDir, "osstack-report-");
  const proofPackCount = countProofPacks(releaseDir);
  const latestReport = latestMarkdownFile(reportDir, "osstack-report-");
  const latestProofPack = latestProofPackArtifact(releaseDir);
  const releaseReadinessBlockers = readReleaseReadinessBlockers(join7(workspacePath, "release-readiness.md"));
  const lessons = listPitfallLessons(opts.db, { limit: 5 });
  const lessonCount = countActivePitfallLessons(opts.db);
  const deployFreezes = listProductionFreezes3(
    opts.db,
    { active_only: true }
  );
  const autonomyReliability = createAutonomyReliabilityReport(opts.db, {
    now: () => new Date(generatedAt * 1e3)
  });
  return {
    dashboard: {
      title: "osstack \u5F00\u53D1\u9A7E\u9A76\u8231",
      version: opts.version,
      phase: opts.phase,
      url: opts.dashboardUrl,
      generated_at: generatedAt,
      db_path: opts.dbPath,
      workspace_path: workspacePath
    },
    overview: {
      progress_percent: progressPercent,
      total_runs: runCounts.total_runs,
      completed_runs: completed,
      failed_runs: failed,
      cancelled_runs: cancelled,
      active_runs: opts.activeRuns,
      queued_tasks: queuedTasks,
      active_loops: opts.activeLoops ?? 0,
      total_events: totalEvents,
      tokens_in: runCounts.tokens_in ?? 0,
      tokens_out: runCounts.tokens_out ?? 0
    },
    board: {
      total: boardTotal,
      latest_event_id: boardView.latest_event_id,
      by_status: byStatus,
      columns: boardColumns.map((col) => ({
        name: col.name,
        cards: col.cards.map((card) => ({
          card_id: card.card_id,
          title: card.title,
          assignee: card.assignee,
          priority: card.priority,
          blocked_reason: card.blocked_reason,
          age_seconds: card.age_seconds
        }))
      }))
    },
    quality: {
      grade,
      success_rate: Number(successRate.toFixed(2)),
      blocked_cards: blockedCards,
      avg_duration_ms: runCounts.avg_duration_ms,
      dimensions: [
        { name: "\u4EFB\u52A1\u5B8C\u6210\u7387", grade, detail: `${Math.round(successRate * 100)}% terminal runs completed` },
        { name: "\u963B\u585E\u5065\u5EB7", grade: blockedCards === 0 ? "A" : "B", detail: `${blockedCards} blocked card(s)` },
        { name: "\u4E8B\u4EF6\u8FFD\u8E2A", grade: totalEvents > 0 || runCounts.total_runs === 0 ? "A" : "B", detail: `${totalEvents} persisted event(s)` },
        { name: "Agent \u8DEF\u7531", grade: opts.adapters.length >= 2 ? "A" : "B", detail: `${opts.adapters.length} registered adapter(s)` }
      ]
    },
    agents: [...opts.adapters],
    agent_scorecards: listAgentScorecards(opts.db, { limit: 5 }),
    evidence: {
      reports: reportsCount,
      proof_packs: proofPackCount,
      latest_report: latestReport,
      latest_proof_pack: latestProofPack
    },
    lessons: {
      total: lessonCount,
      recent: lessons.map((lesson) => ({
        pitfall_id: lesson.pitfall_id,
        family: lesson.family,
        symptom: lesson.symptom,
        verified_count: lesson.verified_count,
        legacy_verified_count: lesson.legacy_verified_count,
        observation_count: lesson.observation_count
      }))
    },
    autonomy: {
      reliability: autonomyReliability,
      deploy_freezes: deployFreezes.map((freeze) => ({
        repo_id: freeze.repo_id,
        target: freeze.target,
        frozen: true,
        reason: freeze.reason,
        source_run_id: freeze.source_run_id,
        frozen_at: freeze.frozen_at
      }))
    },
    recent_runs: recentRuns,
    next_steps: [
      ...releaseReadinessNextSteps(releaseReadinessBlockers, workspacePath),
      {
        label: "\u4EA7\u54C1\u8BBE\u8BA1",
        detail: "google-stitch 2.0 primary, claude-design fallback",
        command: "osstack guide --write",
        status: "ready"
      },
      {
        label: "Coding",
        detail: "codex-cli primary, claude-code fallback",
        command: 'osstack deliver "<task>" --auto-agent --worktree --checkpoint',
        status: opts.activeRuns > 0 ? "running" : "ready"
      },
      {
        label: "Loop Engineering",
        detail: `${opts.activeLoops ?? 0} active loop session(s)`,
        command: "osstack cockpit run --workspace <path> --json",
        status: (opts.activeLoops ?? 0) > 0 ? "running" : "ready"
      },
      {
        label: "\u6D4B\u8BD5\u9A8C\u8BC1",
        detail: "claude-code primary, codex-cli fallback",
        command: "osstack cockpit proof --workspace <path> --json",
        status: blockedCards > 0 ? "blocked" : "ready"
      }
    ]
  };
}
function dirnameOfDb(dbPath) {
  return dbPath === ":memory:" ? "." : dirname7(dbPath);
}
function countActivePitfallLessons(db2) {
  const row = db2.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM pitfall_lessons WHERE status = 'active'").get();
  return row?.total ?? 0;
}
function countMarkdownFiles(dir, prefix) {
  try {
    return readdirSync2(dir).filter((name) => name.startsWith(prefix) && name.endsWith(".md")).length;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}
function countProofPacks(dir) {
  try {
    return readdirSync2(dir).filter((name) => {
      if (!name.startsWith("osstack-proof-")) return false;
      try {
        return statSync(join7(dir, name)).isDirectory();
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    }).length;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}
function latestMarkdownFile(dir, prefix) {
  return latestDirectoryEntry(dir, (name) => name.startsWith(prefix) && name.endsWith(".md"));
}
function latestProofPackArtifact(dir) {
  const artifact = latestDirectoryEntry(dir, (name, path) => {
    if (!name.startsWith("osstack-proof-")) return false;
    try {
      return statSync(path).isDirectory();
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  });
  if (artifact === null) return null;
  const manifest = join7(artifact.path, "manifest.json");
  try {
    statSync(manifest);
    return { ...artifact, manifest };
  } catch {
    return { ...artifact, manifest: null };
  }
}
function latestDirectoryEntry(dir, accept) {
  try {
    let latest = null;
    for (const name of readdirSync2(dir)) {
      const path = join7(dir, name);
      if (!accept(name, path)) continue;
      let artifact;
      try {
        const stats = statSync(path);
        artifact = {
          name,
          path,
          updated_at: stats.mtimeMs / 1e3
        };
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      if (latest === null || artifact.updated_at > latest.updated_at) latest = artifact;
    }
    return latest;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function readReleaseReadinessBlockers(path) {
  let markdown;
  try {
    markdown = readFileSync6(path, "utf-8");
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
  const blockers = [];
  let inBlockers = false;
  for (const line of markdown.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    if (/^##\s+Blockers\s*$/i.test(line.trim())) {
      inBlockers = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (inBlockers && /^##\s+/.test(line.trim())) break;
    if (!inBlockers) continue;
    const match = line.match(/^-\s+\[([^\]]+)\]\s+(.+)$/);
    if (match) blockers.push({ id: match[1].trim(), detail: match[2].trim() });
  }
  return blockers;
}
function releaseReadinessNextSteps(blockers, workspacePath) {
  if (blockers.length === 0) return [];
  const ids = blockers.map((blocker) => blocker.id).join(", ");
  return [{
    label: "Release Readiness",
    detail: `${blockers.length} blocker(s): ${ids}`,
    command: `osstack release doctor --target all --workspace ${shellArg(workspacePath)} --output release-readiness.md --json`,
    status: "blocked",
    blockers
  }];
}
function shellArg(value) {
  if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_./:-]+$/.test(value)) return value;
  return `'${value.replaceAll("'", "'\\''")}'`;
}
async function startDashboardServer(snapshot2, opts = dashboardUrlFromEnv()) {
  let url = dashboardUrl(opts);
  const server = createServer((req, res) => {
    void handleDashboardRequest(req, res, snapshot2, url);
  });
  await new Promise((resolve4, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    server.listen(opts.port, opts.host, () => {
      server.off("error", reject);
      resolve4();
    });
  });
  const addr = server.address();
  const port = addr?.port ?? opts.port;
  const resolvedOpts = { ...opts, port };
  url = dashboardUrl(resolvedOpts);
  return {
    url,
    host: opts.host,
    port,
    close: () => new Promise((resolve4) => server.close(() => resolve4()))
  };
}
async function handleDashboardRequest(req, res, snapshot2, url) {
  const requestUrl = new URL(req.url ?? "/", url);
  const path = requestUrl.pathname;
  if (path === "/" || path === "/dashboard") {
    send(res, 200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", renderDashboardHtml());
    return;
  }
  if (path === "/api/dashboard") {
    const workspacePath = requestUrl.searchParams.get("workspace") ?? void 0;
    send(res, 200, "application/json; charset=utf-8", JSON.stringify(snapshot2(
      workspacePath === void 0 ? void 0 : { workspacePath }
    )));
    return;
  }
  if (path === "/dashboard/url") {
    send(res, 200, "application/json; charset=utf-8", JSON.stringify({
      url: dashboardUrlWithWorkspace(url, requestUrl.searchParams.get("workspace") ?? void 0)
    }));
    return;
  }
  send(res, 404, "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "not found");
}
function dashboardUrlWithWorkspace(url, workspacePath) {
  if (workspacePath === void 0) return url;
  const nextUrl = new URL(url);
  nextUrl.searchParams.set("workspace", workspacePath);
  return nextUrl.toString();
}
function send(res, status, contentType, body) {
  res.writeHead(status, { "Content-Type": contentType, "Cache-Control": "no-store" });
  res.end(body);
}
function dashboardLanguage(value = process.env["OSSTACK_LANG"]) {
  const normalized = value?.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (normalized === "zh-cn" || normalized === "zh" || normalized === "cn") return "zh-CN";
  return "en";
}

    return {

      title: "osstack \u5F00\u53D1\u9A7E\u9A76\u8231",
      subtitle: "\u4EA7\u54C1\u8BBE\u8BA1 \xB7 \u7F16\u7801 \xB7 \u6D4B\u8BD5\u9A8C\u8BC1 \xB7 \u4EFB\u52A1\u770B\u677F \xB7 \u8D28\u91CF\u8FFD\u8E2A",
      agentRoles: "Agent \u5206\u5DE5",
      quality: "\u8D28\u91CF\u9762\u677F",

function renderDashboardHtml(language = dashboardLanguage()) {
  const text = dashboardText(language);
  return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="${text.htmlLang}">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>${text.title}</title>
<style>
  :root { color-scheme: dark; --bg:#0b1020; --panel:#111827; --panel2:#172033; --line:#263247; --text:#eef2ff; --muted:#94a3b8; --blue:#38bdf8; --green:#22c55e; --yellow:#f59e0b; --red:#ef4444; }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body { margin:0; background:var(--bg); color:var(--text); font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; }
  main { max-width:1180px; margin:0 auto; padding:28px 20px 40px; }
  header { display:flex; align-items:flex-start; justify-content:space-between; gap:16px; margin-bottom:22px; }
  h1 { font-size:22px; margin:0 0 4px; letter-spacing:0; }
  h2 { font-size:12px; color:var(--muted); font-weight:600; margin:0 0 12px; text-transform:uppercase; }
  .sub { color:var(--muted); font-size:13px; margin:0; }
  .brand { display:flex; gap:12px; align-items:center; }
  .mark { width:38px; height:38px; border-radius:8px; background:#0ea5e9; display:grid; place-items:center; font-weight:800; }
  .pill { font-size:11px; border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:999px; padding:5px 9px; color:var(--muted); white-space:nowrap; }
  .grid6 { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap:12px; }
  .grid3 { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap:14px; }
  .grid2 { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap:14px; }
  .panel { background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--panel2),var(--panel)); border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.12); border-radius:8px; padding:16px; }
  .metric .label { color:var(--muted); font-size:11px; }
  .metric .value { margin-top:4px; font-size:26px; font-weight:800; }
  .bar { height:6px; background:#334155; border-radius:99px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:10px; }
  .fill { height:100%; background:var(--blue); width:0; transition:width .4s ease; }
  .section { margin-top:22px; }
  .list { display:grid; gap:9px; }
  .row { display:flex; align-items:flex-start; gap:10px; font-size:12px; color:#cbd5e1; }
  .dot { width:8px; height:8px; border-radius:999px; margin-top:5px; background:var(--muted); flex:0 0 auto; }
  .ok { color:var(--green); } .warn { color:var(--yellow); } .err { color:var(--red); } .blue { color:var(--blue); }
  .dot.ok { background:var(--green); } .dot.warn { background:var(--yellow); } .dot.err { background:var(--red); } .dot.blue { background:var(--blue); }
  .tag { font-size:10px; border-radius:4px; padding:2px 6px; background:#1e293b; color:var(--muted); }
  .quality { width:38px; height:38px; border-radius:999px; display:grid; place-items:center; background:#1e293b; font-weight:800; }
  .cards { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(128px, 1fr)); gap:10px; overflow:auto; padding-bottom:2px; }
  .lane { min-height:168px; border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:8px; padding:10px; background:rgba(15,23,42,.45); }
  .lane-title { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; color:var(--muted); font-size:11px; margin-bottom:8px; }
  .card { border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.15); background:#0f172a; border-radius:6px; padding:9px; margin-bottom:8px; font-size:12px; }
  .card small { color:var(--muted); display:block; margin-top:4px; }
  table { width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:12px; }
  th, td { border-bottom:1px solid var(--line); text-align:left; padding:9px 6px; color:#cbd5e1; }
  th { color:var(--muted); font-weight:600; }
  code { display:inline-block; margin-top:4px; padding:2px 5px; border-radius:4px; background:#020617; color:#bae6fd; font-size:11px; }
  .release-blocker { display:block; margin-top:5px; color:#fca5a5; font-size:11px; line-height:1.35; }
  @media (max-width: 900px) { .grid6, .grid3, .grid2 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } header { flex-direction:column; } }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<main>
  <header>
    <div class="brand">
      <div class="mark">OS</div>
      <div>
        <h1>${text.title}</h1>
        <p class="sub">${text.subtitle}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pill" id="generated">loading...</div>
  </header>

  <section class="grid6" id="metrics"></section>

  <section class="section grid3">
    <div class="panel">
      <h2>${text.agentRoles}</h2>
      <div class="list" id="agents"></div>
    </div>

      <div id="quality"></div>
    </div>

      <div class="list" id="next"></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section grid3">
    <div class="panel">
      <h2>${text.evidence}</h2>
      <div class="list" id="evidence"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel">
      <h2>${text.scorecards}</h2>
      <div class="list" id="scorecards"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel">
      <h2>${text.lessons}</h2>
      <div class="list" id="lessons"></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section panel">
    <h2>${text.kanban}</h2>
    <div class="cards" id="board"></div>
  </section>

  <section class="section grid2">
    <div class="panel">
      <h2>${text.recentRuns}</h2>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Run</th><th>Agent</th><th>Phase</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody id="runs"></tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    <div class="panel">

function esc(s){ return String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }
function metric(label, value, color, pct){
  return '<div class="panel metric"><div class="label">'+label+'</div><div class="value '+color+'">'+value+'</div>'+(pct !== undefined ? '<div class="bar"><div class="fill" style="width:'+pct+'%"></div></div>' : '')+'</div>';
}
function statusClass(status){ return status === "done" || status === "completed" ? "ok" : status === "blocked" || status === "failed" ? "err" : status === "running" ? "warn" : "blue"; }
function releaseBlockersHtml(n){
  const blockers = Array.isArray(n.blockers) ? n.blockers : [];
  return blockers.map(b => '<span class="release-blocker">blocker '+esc(b.id)+': '+esc(b.detail)+'</span>').join('');
}
function artifactRow(label, artifact){
  if (!artifact || !artifact.path) return '';
  return '<div class="row"><span class="dot ok"></span><span><b>'+esc(label)+'</b><br><small>'+esc(artifact.path)+'</small></span></div>';
}

// packages/cli/src/loop-neat-finalizer.ts
import {
  checkKnowledgeHygieneV2
} from "@osstack/governance";
async function runLoopNeatFinalizer(input, deps = {}) {
  if (!input.enabled || !input.loop_succeeded) return void 0;
  try {
    const report = deps.check === void 0 ? await checkKnowledgeHygieneV2({
      schema_version: "2",
      repo_root: input.workspace_path,
      mode: "all"
    }) : await deps.check({
      repo_root: input.workspace_path,
      mode: "all"
    });
    return {
      command: "knowledge_hygiene.check",
      status: "ok",
      report
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      command: "knowledge_hygiene.check",
      status: "error",
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
    };
  }
}

// packages/cli/src/daemon.ts
var DAEMON_VERSION = VERSION;
var DAEMON_PHASE = "S1.5-controls";
var LOOP_FAILURE_STREAK_THRESHOLD = 3;
var DAILY_FAILURE_STOP_THRESHOLD = 5;
function singleOk(id, result) {
  return { id, ok: true, result };
}
function singleErr(id, code, message) {
  return { id, ok: false, error: { code, message } };
}
var TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["completed", "failed", "cancelled"]);
function publicSession(row) {
  if (row === void 0) return null;
  return {
    session_id: row.session_id,
    task_id: row.task_id,
    run_id: row.run_id,
    agent_id: row.agent_id,
    runtime_id: row.agent_id,
    state: row.state,
    resume_supported: row.resume_supported,
    resume_ready: row.resume_supported && row.native_session_id !== null && !TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES.has(row.state),
    version: row.version,
    updated_at: row.updated_at
  };
}
function publicSessionWait(ctx, sessionId) {
  const row = ctx.sessions.getSession(sessionId);
  if (row === void 0) return null;
  const snapshot2 = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(sessionId);
  const pending = ctx.db.prepare(`
    SELECT message_id, state FROM session_messages
    WHERE session_id = ? AND direction = 'inbound'
      AND (state = 'accepted' OR (state = 'failed' AND turn_id = ?))
    ORDER BY CASE WHEN state = 'accepted' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, created_at DESC, message_id LIMIT 1
  `).get(sessionId, snapshot2.turn_id);
  const wait = describeSessionWait({
    state: row.state,
    decision: snapshot2.decision,
    resume_ready: row.resume_supported && row.native_session_id !== null,
    applying: ctx.activeSessionSends.has(sessionId),
    ...pending === void 0 ? {} : { pending_message: pending }
  });
  const grant = ctx.continuations.forSession(sessionId);
  if (wait?.reason === "phase_continue" && grant?.status === "active" && Date.parse(grant.body.expires_at) > Date.now() && ctx.collaborator?.isScheduled(grant.grant_id)) {
    return { ...wait, automatic_continuation: true, grant_id: grant.grant_id };
  }
  return wait;
}
function publicCollaborator(ctx, sessionId) {
  const grant = ctx.continuations.forSession(sessionId);
  return grant === void 0 ? null : {
    grant_id: grant.grant_id,
    status: grant.status,
    reason: grant.reason,
    step_index: grant.step_index,
    steps_total: grant.body.steps.length,
    checks_used: grant.checks_used,
    max_checks: grant.body.max_checks,
    resumes_used: grant.resumes_used,
    max_resumes: grant.body.max_resumes,
    expires_at: grant.body.expires_at,
    scheduled: ctx.collaborator?.isScheduled(grant.grant_id) ?? false
  };
}
function parseDecisionDirective(text) {
  let root2;
  try {
    root2 = JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return void 0;
  }
  if (typeof root2 !== "object" || root2 === null || Array.isArray(root2)) return void 0;
  const outer = root2;
  if (Object.keys(outer).length !== 1 || !("ostag_decision" in outer)) return void 0;
  const parsed = SessionDecision2.safeParse(outer["ostag_decision"]);
  return parsed.success ? { type: "decision.requested", ...parsed.data } : void 0;
}
function parseSessionStartParams(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {
    return new Error("params must be an object");
  }
  const p = value;
  if (typeof p["request_id"] !== "string" || p["request_id"].trim() === "" || p["request_id"].length > 256) {
    return new Error("params.request_id must be a non-empty string of at most 256 characters");
  }
  if (typeof p["prompt"] !== "string" || p["prompt"].trim() === "" || p["prompt"].length > 16e3) {
    return new Error("params.prompt must be between 1 and 16000 characters");
  }
  if (typeof p["title"] !== "string" || p["title"].trim() === "" || p["title"].length > 160) {
    return new Error("params.title must be between 1 and 160 characters");
  }
  if (typeof p["agent_id"] !== "string" || p["agent_id"].trim() === "" || p["agent_id"].length > 256) {
    return new Error("params.agent_id must be a non-empty string of at most 256 characters");
  }
  if (typeof p["repo_path"] !== "string" || !isAbsolute2(p["repo_path"]) || p["repo_path"].length > 4096) {
    return new Error("params.repo_path must be an absolute path of at most 4096 characters");
  }
  return {
    request_id: p["request_id"],
    prompt: p["prompt"],
    title: p["title"],
    agent_id: p["agent_id"],
    repo_path: p["repo_path"]
  };
}
async function handleSessionStart(ctx, req) {
  const p = parseSessionStartParams(req.params);
  if (p instanceof Error) return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", p.message);
  if (ctx.draining) return singleErr(req.id, "draining", "daemon is not accepting new sessions");
  const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(p.agent_id);
  if (adapter === void 0 || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter)) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resumable sessions");
  }
  const capabilities = adapter.sessionCapabilities();
  if (!capabilities.resume) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resume");
  }
  const availability = await adapter.isAvailable();
  if (!availability.available) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "agent_unavailable", "agent is unavailable");
  }
  const now = Date.now() / 1e3;
  const stableId = createHash5("sha256").update(p.request_id).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
  const taskId = `task_${stableId}`;
  const runId = `run_${stableId}`;
  const sessionId = `sess_${stableId}`;
  const turnId = `turn_${randomUUID3()}`;
  const requestSha256 = createHash5("sha256").update(JSON.stringify({
    request_id: p.request_id,
    prompt: p.prompt,
    title: p.title,
    agent_id: p.agent_id,
    repo_path: p.repo_path
  })).digest("hex");
  const existing = ctx.sessions.getSession(sessionId);
  if (existing !== void 0) {
    if (existing.request_sha256 !== requestSha256) {
      return singleErr(
        req.id,
        "request_id_conflict",
        "request_id was already used for a different session request"
      );
    }
    const snapshot2 = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(sessionId);
    return singleOk(req.id, {
      ...publicSession(existing),
      ...snapshot2,
      events: []
    });
  }
  try {
    ctx.db.transaction(() => {
      insertTask(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, { task_id: taskId, title: p.title, created_at: now });
      insertRun(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, {
        run_id: runId,
        task_id: taskId,
        session_id: sessionId,
        agent_id: adapter.id,
        capability_id: `${adapter.id}.session`,
        phase: "code",
        started_at: now
      });
      ctx.sessions.createSession({
        session_id: sessionId,
        task_id: taskId,
        run_id: runId,
        agent_id: adapter.id,
        request_sha256: requestSha256,
        native_session_id: null,
        repo_path: p.repo_path,
        state: "starting",
        resume_supported: true,
        version: 1,
        updated_at: now
      });
      ctx.kanban.createCard({
        card_id: `card_${runId}`,
        task_id: taskId,
        title: p.title,
        body: `agent: ${adapter.id}
capability: resumable-session`,
        status: "running",
        assignee: adapter.id
      });
    })();
  } catch {
    return singleErr(req.id, "store_write_failed", "could not create resumable session");
  }
  const controller = new AbortController();
  ctx.activeRuns.set(runId, controller);
  const task = {
    run_id: runId,
    task_id: taskId,
    session_id: sessionId,
    capability_id: `${adapter.id}.session`,
    prompt: p.prompt,
    context: { repo_path: p.repo_path }
  };
  const publicEvents = [];
  let nativeSessionId;
  let assistantText = "";
  let decision = null;
  let lease;
  let started = false;
  let settled = false;
  try {
    lease = await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, adapter.id, p.repo_path, runId, controller.signal);
    started = true;
    for await (const rawEvent of boundedAdapterEvents(adapter.startSession(task, controller.signal), controller.signal)) {
      const event = AgentSessionEventSchema.parse(rawEvent);
      if (event.type === "native_session.started") {
        if (nativeSessionId !== void 0 && nativeSessionId !== event.native_session_id) {
          throw new Error("adapter changed native session id");
        }
        nativeSessionId = event.native_session_id;
        continue;
      }
      if (nativeSessionId !== void 0 && JSON.stringify(event).includes(nativeSessionId)) {
        throw new Error("adapter output contained protected session metadata");
      }
      if (event.type === "assistant.message") {
        const directive = isControlledSessionAdapter2(adapter) ? void 0 : parseDecisionDirective(event.text);
        if (directive !== void 0) {
          publicEvents.push(directive);
          decision = {
            decision_id: directive.decision_id,
            question: directive.question,
            choices: directive.choices,
            ...directive.continuation === void 0 ? {} : { continuation: directive.continuation }
          };
          assistantText = directive.question;
          continue;
        }
        assistantText += event.text;
      }
      publicEvents.push(event);
      if (assistantText.length > 4e3) throw new Error("assistant output exceeded relay bound");
      if (event.type === "decision.requested") decision = {
        decision_id: event.decision_id,
        question: event.question,
        choices: event.choices,
        ...event.continuation === void 0 ? {} : { continuation: event.continuation }
      };
      if (event.type === "session.terminal" && event.state !== "completed") {
        throw new Error("adapter terminated while starting the session");
      }
    }
    settled = true;
    if (nativeSessionId === void 0) throw new Error("adapter did not return a native session id");
    const confirmedNativeSessionId = nativeSessionId;
    const finalState = decision === null ? "idle" : "awaiting_decision";
    const completedAt = Date.now() / 1e3;
    const updated = ctx.db.transaction(() => {
      const recorded = ctx.sessions.recordNativeSession(
        sessionId,
        confirmedNativeSessionId,
        1,
        completedAt
      );
      ctx.sessions.appendTurn({
        turn_id: turnId,
        session_id: sessionId,
        ordinal: 1,
        state: finalState,
        assistant_text: assistantText,
        decision,
        created_at: now,
        updated_at: completedAt
      });
      const session = ctx.sessions.setState(
        sessionId,
        finalState,
        recorded.version,
        completedAt
      );
      finalizeRun(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, runId, "completed", completedAt);
      ctx.kanban.moveCard(`card_${runId}`, "done", { actor: adapter.id });
      return session;
    })();
    return singleOk(req.id, {
      ...publicSession(updated),
      turn_id: turnId,
      assistant_text: assistantText,
      decision,
      events: publicEvents
    });
  } catch {
    try {
      const current2 = ctx.sessions.getSession(sessionId);
      if (current2 !== void 0) {
        ctx.sessions.setState(sessionId, "failed", current2.version, Date.now() / 1e3);
      }
      finalizeRun(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, runId, "failed", Date.now() / 1e3);
      ctx.kanban.moveCard(`card_${runId}`, "blocked", { actor: adapter.id, blocked_reason: "session start failed" });
    } catch {
    }
    return singleErr(req.id, "agent_failed", "agent failed while starting the session");
  } finally {
    if (started && (!settled || controller.signal.aborted)) {
      controller.abort();
      if (await confirmAdapterStopped(adapter, runId)) await lease?.release();
      else lease?.retain("session start exit unconfirmed");
    } else await lease?.release();
    ctx.activeRuns.delete(runId);
  }
}
function parseSessionSendParams(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {
    return new Error("params must be an object");
  }
  const p = value;
  for (const key of ["session_id", "runtime_id", "turn_id", "message_id"]) {
    if (typeof p[key] !== "string" || p[key].trim() === "" || p[key].length > 256) {
      return new Error(`params.${key} must be a non-empty string of at most 256 characters`);
    }
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(p["expected_version"]) || p["expected_version"] < 1) {
    return new Error("params.expected_version must be a positive integer");
  }
  if (typeof p["text"] !== "string" || p["text"].trim() === "" || p["text"].length > 16e3) {
    return new Error("params.text must be between 1 and 16000 characters");
  }
  if (p["requested_by"] !== void 0 && (typeof p["requested_by"] !== "string" || p["requested_by"].trim() === "" || p["requested_by"].length > 256)) {
    return new Error("params.requested_by must be a non-empty string of at most 256 characters");
  }
  return {
    session_id: p["session_id"],
    runtime_id: p["runtime_id"],
    turn_id: p["turn_id"],
    message_id: p["message_id"],
    expected_version: p["expected_version"],
    text: p["text"],
    ...p["requested_by"] === void 0 ? {} : { requested_by: p["requested_by"] }
  };
}
async function handleSessionSend(ctx, req) {
  const p = parseSessionSendParams(req.params);
  if (p instanceof Error) return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", p.message);
  if (ctx.draining && ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state !== "applied") {
    return singleErr(req.id, "draining", "daemon is not accepting session instructions");
  }
  const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id);
  if (session === void 0) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
  if (p.runtime_id !== session.agent_id) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "runtime_mismatch", "session belongs to another runtime");
  }
  const existingMessage = ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id);
  let recoveredReservationVersion;
  if (existingMessage !== void 0) {
    try {
      const replay = ctx.sessions.applyInbound({
        message_id: p.message_id,
        session_id: p.session_id,
        turn_id: p.turn_id,
        direction: "inbound",
        kind: "instruction.send",
        payload: { text: p.text, ...p.requested_by === void 0 ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by } },
        state: "accepted",
        created_at: Date.now() / 1e3
      }, p.expected_version, Date.now() / 1e3);
      if (existingMessage.state === "applied") {
        return singleOk(req.id, { state: replay.outcome, session_version: replay.session_version });
      }
      if (replay.session_version !== p.expected_version + 1) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "accepted message cannot be recovered safely");
      }
      if (existingMessage.state === "accepted") {
        return ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id) ? singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction") : singleErr(
          req.id,
          "delivery_uncertain",
          "the previous delivery may have reached the agent; inspect the session before using a new message_id"
        );
      }
      if (existingMessage.state !== "failed") {
        return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "reserved message is not retryable");
      }
      ctx.sessions.retryFailedMessage(p.message_id);
      recoveredReservationVersion = replay.session_version;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
  if (recoveredReservationVersion === void 0 && TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES.has(session.state)) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "session_terminal", `session state=${session.state}`);
  }
  if (!session.resume_supported || session.native_session_id === null) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "session cannot be resumed");
  }
  const snapshot2 = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(p.session_id);
  if (recoveredReservationVersion === void 0 && snapshot2.turn_id !== p.turn_id) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "turn_conflict", "message does not target the current turn");
  }
  const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(session.agent_id);
  if (adapter === void 0 || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter) || !adapter.sessionCapabilities().resume) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resume");
  }
  if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) {
    return singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction");
  }
  let applied;
  const now = Date.now() / 1e3;
  if (recoveredReservationVersion !== void 0) {
    applied = { outcome: "applied", session_version: recoveredReservationVersion };
  } else {
    try {
      applied = ctx.sessions.applyInbound({
        message_id: p.message_id,
        session_id: p.session_id,
        turn_id: p.turn_id,
        direction: "inbound",
        kind: "instruction.send",
        payload: { text: p.text, ...p.requested_by === void 0 ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by } },
        state: "accepted",
        created_at: now
      }, p.expected_version, now);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof SessionVersionConflict) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "session version changed; refresh before retrying");
      }
      if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
  if (applied.outcome === "duplicate") {
    return singleOk(req.id, { state: "duplicate", session_version: applied.session_version });
  }
  return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, req, p, session, adapter, applied.session_version);
}
async function executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, req, p, session, adapter, reservationVersion, options = {}) {
  if (session.native_session_id === null) return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "session cannot resume");
  const applied = { session_version: reservationVersion };
  ctx.activeSessionSends.add(session.session_id);
  const controller = options.controller ?? new AbortController();
  const now = Date.now() / 1e3;
  ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
  let lease;
  let started = false;
  let settled = false;
  try {
    lease = await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, adapter.id, session.repo_path, session.run_id, controller.signal);
    const failReservedMessage = (code, message) => {
      try {
        ctx.sessions.markMessageFailed(p.message_id);
      } catch {
        return singleErr(
          req.id,
          "delivery_uncertain",
          "the agent failed and the reservation could not be finalized; inspect the session before retrying"
        );
      }
      return singleErr(req.id, code, message);
    };
    const context = {};
    if (session.repo_path !== "") context["repo_path"] = session.repo_path;
    if (p.requested_by !== void 0) context["requested_by"] = p.requested_by;
    const input = {
      session_id: session.session_id,
      task_id: session.task_id,
      run_id: session.run_id,
      turn_id: p.turn_id,
      native_session_id: session.native_session_id,
      expected_version: applied.session_version,
      message_id: p.message_id,
      prompt: p.text,
      ...Object.keys(context).length === 0 ? {} : { context }
    };
    const publicEvents = [];
    let assistantText = "";
    let decision = null;
    let terminalCompleted = false;
    try {
      started = true;
      for await (const rawEvent of boundedAdapterEvents(adapter.resumeSession(input, controller.signal), controller.signal)) {
        const event = AgentSessionEventSchema.parse(rawEvent);
        if (event.type === "native_session.started") {
          if (event.native_session_id !== session.native_session_id) {
            return failReservedMessage(
              "adapter_protocol",
              "agent resumed an unexpected native session"
            );
          }
          continue;
        }
        if (JSON.stringify(event).includes(session.native_session_id)) {
          return failReservedMessage(
            "adapter_protocol",
            "agent output contained protected session metadata"
          );
        }
        if (event.type === "session.terminal") {
          if (event.state !== "completed") {
            const rawCode = event.error_code ?? "unknown";
            const safeCode = /^[a-z0-9_]{1,64}$/.test(rawCode) ? rawCode : "unknown";
            return failReservedMessage(
              "agent_failed",
              `agent failed while applying the instruction (${safeCode})`
            );
          }
          terminalCompleted = true;
        }
        if (event.type === "assistant.message") {
          const directive = isControlledSessionAdapter2(adapter) ? void 0 : parseDecisionDirective(event.text);
          if (directive !== void 0) {
            publicEvents.push(directive);
            decision = {
              decision_id: directive.decision_id,
              question: directive.question,
              choices: directive.choices,
              ...directive.continuation === void 0 ? {} : { continuation: directive.continuation }
            };
            assistantText = directive.question;
            continue;
          }
          assistantText += event.text;
        }
        publicEvents.push(event);
        if (event.type === "decision.requested") decision = {
          decision_id: event.decision_id,
          question: event.question,
          choices: event.choices,
          ...event.continuation === void 0 ? {} : { continuation: event.continuation }
        };
      }
      settled = true;
      if (!terminalCompleted) {
        return failReservedMessage(
          "agent_failed",
          "agent ended without completing the instruction"
        );
      }
    } catch {
      return failReservedMessage(
        "agent_failed",
        "agent failed while applying the instruction"
      );
    }
    const finalState = publicEvents.some((event) => event.type === "decision.requested") ? "awaiting_decision" : "idle";
    const completedAt = Date.now() / 1e3;
    const turnId = `turn_${randomUUID3()}`;
    const updatedSession = ctx.db.transaction(() => {
      const updated = ctx.sessions.completeInboundTurn({
        turn: {
          turn_id: turnId,
          session_id: session.session_id,
          ordinal: ctx.db.prepare(
            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal), 0) + 1 AS ordinal FROM session_turns WHERE session_id = ?"
          ).get(session.session_id).ordinal,
          state: finalState,
          assistant_text: assistantText,
          decision,
          created_at: now,
          updated_at: completedAt
        },
        message_id: p.message_id,
        expected_version: applied.session_version,
        applied_at: completedAt
      });
      options.completed?.(turnId, updated.version, completedAt);
      return updated;
    })();
    return singleOk(req.id, {
      state: "applied",
      session_version: updatedSession.version,
      events: publicEvents
    });
  } catch (error) {
    ctx.sessions.markMessageFailed(p.message_id);
    return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  } finally {
    if (started && (!settled || controller.signal.aborted)) {
      controller.abort();
      if (await confirmAdapterStopped(adapter, session.run_id)) await lease?.release();
      else lease?.retain("session resume exit unconfirmed");
    } else await lease?.release();
    ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
    if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
  }
}
async function handleSingle(ctx, req) {
  switch (req.method) {
    case "version":
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        version: DAEMON_VERSION,
        phase: DAEMON_PHASE,
        protocol_version: PROTOCOL_VERSION3
      });
    case "health": {
      const update = readUpdateCache();
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        version: DAEMON_VERSION,
        phase: DAEMON_PHASE,
        protocol_version: PROTOCOL_VERSION3,
        pid: process.pid,
        uptime_s: Math.round(process.uptime()),
        memory_rss_mb: Math.round(process.memoryUsage.rss() / 1024 / 1024),
        db_path: ctx.dbPath,
        adapters: Array.from(ctx.adapters.keys()),
        active_runs: ctx.activeRuns.size,
        draining: ctx.draining,
        concurrency: {
          writes: {
            limit: ctx.writeSemaphore.capacity,
            active: ctx.writeSemaphore.in_use,
            queued: ctx.writeSemaphore.pending
          },
          reads: {
            limit: ctx.readSemaphore.capacity,
            active: ctx.readSemaphore.in_use,
            queued: ctx.readSemaphore.pending
          },
          repository_write_queues: ctx.repoWriteSemaphores.size
        },
        active_loops: activeLoopCount(ctx),
        otel_enabled: otelEnabled(),
        hermes_enabled: ctx.hermes.enabled,
        gbrain_enabled: ctx.gbrain.enabled,
        dashboard_url: ctx.dashboardUrl,
        update_available: update?.update_available ?? false,
        latest_version: update?.latest ?? null
      });
    }
    case "update.status":
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        current: DAEMON_VERSION,
        cache: readUpdateCache()
      });
    case "daemon.drain": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      const timeoutMs = Math.min(Math.max(p.timeout_ms ?? 3e4, 0), 3e5);
      ctx.draining = true;
      ctx.drainingFromHealth = false;
      const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
      while (ctx.activeRuns.size > 0 && Date.now() < deadline) {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
      }
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        draining: true,
        drained: ctx.activeRuns.size === 0,
        active_runs: ctx.activeRuns.size
      });
    }
    case "daemon.resume":
      ctx.draining = false;
      ctx.drainingFromHealth = false;
      ctx.collaborator?.resumePending();
      return singleOk(req.id, { draining: false });
    case "daemon.shutdown":
      setTimeout(() => ctx.requestShutdown(), 50);
      return singleOk(req.id, { shutting_down: true, pid: process.pid });
    case "agent.list":
      return singleOk(
        req.id,
        await Promise.all(
          Array.from(ctx.adapters.values()).map(async (a) => ({
            id: a.id,
            display_name: a.display_name,
            cost_tier: a.cost_tier,
            available: await a.isAvailable(),
            capabilities: a.capabilities().map((c) => ({ id: c.id, kind: c.kind }))
          }))
        )
      );
    case "run.status": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      if (typeof p.run_id !== "string") return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "params.run_id required");
      const row = ctx.db.prepare(
        `SELECT r.run_id, r.task_id, r.session_id, r.agent_id, r.capability_id, r.phase,
                r.outcome, r.started_at, r.completed_at, r.duration_ms,
                r.tokens_in, r.tokens_out, r.retry_count, r.human_intervention_count,
                r.pii_redacted_count, t.title, t.status AS task_status
         FROM runs r JOIN tasks t ON t.task_id = r.task_id
         WHERE r.run_id = ?`
      ).get(p.run_id);
      if (row === void 0) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", `no run with id=${p.run_id}`);
      const active = ctx.activeRuns.has(p.run_id);
      return singleOk(req.id, { ...row, active });
    }
    case "session.list": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      const limit = p.limit ?? 100;
      if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "params.limit must be an integer between 1 and 200");
      }
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        sessions: ctx.sessions.listSessions({ limit }).map((row) => ({
          ...publicSession(row),
          wait: publicSessionWait(ctx, row.session_id),
          collaborator: publicCollaborator(ctx, row.session_id)
        }))
      });
    }
    case "session.start":
      return await handleSessionStart(ctx, req);
    case "session.status": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      if (typeof p.session_id !== "string" || p.session_id.trim() === "") {
        return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "params.session_id required");
      }
      const session = publicSession(ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id));
      return session === null ? singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found") : singleOk(req.id, { ...session, wait: publicSessionWait(ctx, p.session_id), collaborator: publicCollaborator(ctx, p.session_id) });
    }
    case "session.snapshot": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      if (typeof p.session_id !== "string" || p.session_id.trim() === "") {
        return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "params.session_id required");
      }
      if (ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id) === void 0) {
        return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
      }
      return singleOk(req.id, {
        ...ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(p.session_id),
        wait: publicSessionWait(ctx, p.session_id),
        collaborator: publicCollaborator(ctx, p.session_id)
      });
    }
    case "collaborator.inspect": {
      const p = req.params;
      if (typeof p?.["session_id"] !== "string") return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "session_id is required");
      const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(p["session_id"]);
      if (session === void 0) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
      try {
        const policy = await ctx.collaborator.policy(session.agent_id);
        return singleOk(req.id, {
          ...ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(session.session_id),
          repo_path: session.repo_path,
          runtime_policy: policy,
          collaborator: ctx.continuations.forSession(session.session_id) ?? null
        });
      } catch {
        return singleErr(req.id, "controlled_runtime_required", "start a claude-controlled-session before installing a continuation grant");
      }
    }
    case "collaborator.install": {
      try {
        return singleOk(req.id, await ctx.collaborator.install(req.params));
      } catch {
        return singleErr(req.id, "grant_rejected", "grant signature, runtime policy, session cursor, or time window is invalid; inspect the session and signed grant");
      }
    }
    case "collaborator.status":
    case "collaborator.revoke": {
      const p = req.params;
      if (typeof p?.["grant_id"] !== "string") return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "grant_id is required");
      const grant = ctx.continuations.get(p["grant_id"]);
      if (grant === void 0) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "grant not found");
      return singleOk(req.id, req.method === "collaborator.revoke" ? ctx.collaborator.revoke(p["grant_id"]) : grant);
    }
    case "session.send":
      return await handleSessionSend(ctx, req);
    case "task.cancel": {
      const p = req.params ?? {};
      if (typeof p.run_id !== "string") return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", "params.run_id required");
      const ac = ctx.activeRuns.get(p.run_id);
      if (!ac) return singleErr(req.id, "not_active", `run_id=${p.run_id} is not currently active`);
      ac.abort();
      return singleOk(req.id, { cancelled: true, run_id: p.run_id });
    }
    case "stats":
      return singleOk(req.id, computeStats(ctx.db, ctx.loopSessions, ctx.rpcLatency));
    case "autonomy.status": {
      const params = req.params ?? {};
      if (typeof params.autonomy_run_id !== "string" || params.autonomy_run_id.length === 0) {
        return singleErr(
          req.id,
          "bad_request",
          "params.autonomy_run_id required"
        );
      }
      const row = getAutonomyRun(ctx.db, params.autonomy_run_id);
      return row === void 0 ? singleErr(
        req.id,
        "not_found",
        `no autonomy run with id=${params.autonomy_run_id}`
      ) : singleOk(req.id, row);
    }
    case "autonomy.health": {
      try {
        const report = await ctx.autonomyHealth();
