function environmentPositiveInteger(name) {
  const raw = process.env[name];
  if (raw === void 0) return void 0;
  if (!/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(raw)) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive integer`);
  }
  return Number(raw);
}
async function restoreGuardedAutonomy(ctx, health = ctx.lastAutonomyHealth) {
  if (ctx.guardedAutonomy === void 0) return;
  if (health?.can_run_local === false) return;
  const githubRecoveryStates = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
    "blocked_delivery",
    "pushing",
    "opening_pr",
    "waiting_ci",
    "merging"
  ]);
  await Promise.all(
    listResumableAutonomyRuns2(ctx.db).filter((row) => row.state !== "awaiting_approval").filter(
      (row) => health?.can_push_deploy !== false || !githubRecoveryStates.has(row.state)
    ).map(async (row) => {
      try {
        await resumeGuardedAutonomy(
          ctx,
          row.autonomy_run_id,
          void 0,
          "restart"
        );
      } catch (error) {
        process.stderr.write(
          `osstack: autonomy recovery failed for ${row.autonomy_run_id}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}
`
        );
      }
    })
  );
}
async function refreshAutonomyHealthAndRecover(ctx, recover, notificationSender) {
  try {
    const maxLogBytes = environmentPositiveInteger(
      "OSSTACK_LOG_MAX_BYTES"
    );
    const maxLogFiles = environmentPositiveInteger(
      "OSSTACK_LOG_MAX_FILES"
    );
    const proofRetentionDays = environmentPositiveInteger(
      "OSSTACK_PROOF_RETENTION_DAYS"
    );
    const proofMinKeep = environmentPositiveInteger(
      "OSSTACK_PROOF_MIN_KEEP"
    );
    await enforceAutonomyRetention({
      home: osstackHome(),
      db: ctx.db,
      ...maxLogBytes === void 0 ? {} : { max_log_bytes: maxLogBytes },
      ...maxLogFiles === void 0 ? {} : { max_log_files: maxLogFiles },
      ...proofRetentionDays === void 0 ? {} : { proof_retention_days: proofRetentionDays },
      ...proofMinKeep === void 0 ? {} : { proof_min_keep: proofMinKeep }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(
      `osstack: autonomy retention failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}
`
    );
  }
  if (notificationSender !== void 0) {
    try {
      await drainAutonomyNotifications({
        db: ctx.db,
        db_path: ctx.dbPath,
        sender: notificationSender
      });
    } catch (error) {
      process.stderr.write(
        `osstack: autonomy notification drain failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}
`
      );
    }
  }
  if (ctx.guardedAutonomy === void 0) return;
  const previous = ctx.lastAutonomyHealth;
  let current2;
  try {
    current2 = await ctx.autonomyHealth();
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(
      `osstack: autonomy health probe failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}
`
    );
    return;
  }
  ctx.lastAutonomyHealth = current2;
  applyAutonomyDrainSignal(ctx, current2.drain_required);
  ctx.collaborator?.resumePending();
  const externalRecovered = previous !== void 0 && !previous.can_push_deploy && current2.can_push_deploy;
  if (recover || externalRecovered) {
    await restoreGuardedAutonomy(ctx, current2);
  }
}
function applyAutonomyDrainSignal(ctx, drainRequired) {
  if (drainRequired) {
    if (!ctx.draining) ctx.drainingFromHealth = true;
    ctx.draining = true;
    return;
  }
  if (ctx.draining && ctx.drainingFromHealth) {
    ctx.draining = false;
    ctx.drainingFromHealth = false;
    ctx.collaborator?.resumePending();
    process.stderr.write("osstack: autonomy health recovered \u2014 daemon resumed accepting work\n");
  }
}
function collaboratorPaused(ctx) {
  return ctx.draining || ctx.guardedAutonomy !== void 0 && (ctx.lastAutonomyHealth === void 0 || !ctx.lastAutonomyHealth.can_run_local || ctx.lastAutonomyHealth.drain_required);
}
async function startDaemon(opts = {}) {
  let checkpointService;
  let checkpointClosed = false;
  const checkpointBindings = new Map(Array.from(opts.graphCheckpointBindings ?? [], ([key, binding]) => {
    const { authorize, ...data } = binding;
    return [key, { ...structuredClone(data), authorize }];
  }));
  let graphBroker;
  let graphRuntime;
  const getCheckpoints = async () => {
    if (checkpointClosed) throw new Error("CHECKPOINT_SERVICE_CLOSED");
    checkpointService ??= Promise.all([Promise.resolve().then(() => (init_graph_checkpoint_service(), graph_checkpoint_service_exports)), import("@osstack/core")]).then(([{ GraphCheckpointService: GraphCheckpointService2 }, { GraphRunStore: GraphRunStore4, GraphCheckpointStore: GraphCheckpointStore2 }]) => new GraphCheckpointService2({ runs: new GraphRunStore4(ctx.db), checkpoints: new GraphCheckpointStore2(ctx.db), bindings: checkpointBindings }));
    const service = await checkpointService;
    if (checkpointClosed) {
      service.close();
      throw new Error("CHECKPOINT_SERVICE_CLOSED");
    }
    return service;
  };
  const getBroker = () => {
    if (checkpointClosed) return Promise.reject(new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_CLOSED"));
    return graphBroker ??= Promise.resolve().then(() => (init_graph_action_broker(), graph_action_broker_exports)).then(({ GraphActionBroker: GraphActionBroker2 }) => new GraphActionBroker2(ctx.db, {
      acquire: (input) => admissionFor(ctx).acquire(input),
      authorizePolicy: async (workspacePath) => {
        const decision = await makeRepositoryPolicyGate(workspacePath, true)({ workspacePath });
        if (!decision.allow) throw new Error(decision.reason);
      }
    }));
  };
  const registeredWorkspace = opts.workspacePath === void 0 ? void 0 : (await resolveRepositoryScope3(opts.workspacePath)).workspacePath;
  const socketPath = opts.socketPath ?? resolveSocketPath();
  const continuationTrust = new Map(opts.continuationTrustedPublicKeys ?? await readContinuationTrust(join8(osstackHome(), "continuation-trust.json")));
  const releaseLock = acquireDaemonLock(pidFilePath());
  cleanupStaleSocket(socketPath);
  await initOtel({ serviceVersion: DAEMON_VERSION });
  const ctx = buildContext({
    socketPath,
    ...opts.adapters === void 0 ? {} : { adapters: opts.adapters },
    ...opts.writeConcurrency === void 0 ? {} : { writeConcurrency: opts.writeConcurrency },
    ...opts.readConcurrency === void 0 ? {} : { readConcurrency: opts.readConcurrency }
  });
  ctx.collaborator = new SessionCollaborator({
    repository: ctx.continuations,
    adapters: ctx.adapters,
    trustedPublicKeys: continuationTrust,
    draining: () => collaboratorPaused(ctx),
    send: async (reservation, controller) => {
      const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(reservation.grant.body.session_id);
      const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(reservation.grant.body.runtime_id);
      if (collaboratorPaused(ctx) || controller.signal.aborted) return { ok: false, error: { code: "continuation_cancelled" } };
      if (session === void 0 || adapter === void 0 || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter)) {
        return { ok: false, error: { code: "resume_unsupported" } };
      }
      if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) return { ok: false, error: { code: "session_busy" } };
      return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, { id: reservation.attempt.attempt_id, method: "collaborator.resume" }, {
        session_id: session.session_id,
        runtime_id: session.agent_id,
        turn_id: reservation.message.turn_id,
        message_id: reservation.message.message_id,
        expected_version: reservation.reservation_version - 1,
        text: reservation.prompt,
        requested_by: `agent:collaborator:${reservation.grant.body.grant_id}`
      }, session, adapter, reservation.reservation_version, {
        controller,
        completed: (turnId, version2, now) => {
          const grant = reservation.grant;
          if (now * 1e3 >= Date.parse(grant.body.expires_at)) ctx.continuations.block(grant.grant_id, "expired", now);
          else if ((now - reservation.message.created_at) * 1e3 >= grant.body.turn_timeout_ms) ctx.continuations.block(grant.grant_id, "turn_timeout", now);
          ctx.continuations.completeAttempt(reservation.attempt.attempt_id, turnId, version2, now);
        }
      });
    }
  });
  if (registeredWorkspace !== void 0 || opts.adapterWorkspaces !== void 0) {
    ctx.adapterWorkspaces = new Map([
      ...registeredWorkspace === void 0 ? [] : [...ctx.adapters.keys()].map((id) => [id, registeredWorkspace]),
      ...opts.adapterWorkspaces ?? []
    ]);
  }
  if (opts.autonomyHealth !== void 0) {
    ctx.autonomyHealth = opts.autonomyHealth;
  }
  if (opts.guardedAutonomyFactory !== void 0) {
    ctx.guardedAutonomy = await opts.guardedAutonomyFactory(
      ctx.db,
      ctx.dbPath
    );
  }
  const stopUpdateChecker = startUpdateChecker();
  let dashboard = null;
  const server = createServer2((sock) => {
    const decoder = new FrameDecoder();
    sock.on("data", (chunk) => {
      try {
        const frames = decoder.push(chunk);
        for (const raw of frames) {
          const req = raw;
          if (typeof req !== "object" || req === null || typeof req.method !== "string") {
            sock.write(encodeFrame(singleErr(req?.id ?? 0, "bad_request", "request must have a 'method' string")));
            continue;
          }
          const startedAt = performance.now();
          const recordLatency = () => {
            ctx.rpcLatency.record(req.method, performance.now() - startedAt);
          };
          const reportFailure = (error) => {
            try {
              sock.write(encodeFrame(singleErr(req.id, "execution_failed", error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error))));
            } catch {
            }
          };
          if (req.method === "task.submit") {
            void traceSpan(
              "osstack.task.submit",
              { "osstack.method": req.method },
              () => handleTaskSubmit(ctx, req, sock)
            ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
            continue;
          }
          if (req.method === "task.observe") {
            void traceSpan(
              "osstack.task.observe",
              { "osstack.method": req.method },
              async () => handleTaskObserve(ctx, req, sock)
            ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
            continue;
          }
          void traceSpan(
            "osstack.rpc",
            { "osstack.method": req.method },
            async () => {
              const resp = await handleSingle(ctx, req);
              if (resp === null) return;
              try {
                sock.write(encodeFrame(resp));
              } catch {
              }
            }
          ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
        try {
          sock.write(encodeFrame({ id: 0, ok: false, error: { code: "framing", message } }));
        } catch {
        }
        sock.destroy();
      }
    });
    sock.on("error", () => {
    });
  });
  return new Promise((resolve4, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    void Promise.all([restoreLoopSessions(ctx), restoreDailySessions(ctx)]).then(() => {
      server.listen(socketPath, () => {
        try {
          chmodSync(socketPath, 384);
        } catch {
        }
        let healthPollPending = true;
        let guardedRecovery = refreshAutonomyHealthAndRecover(
          ctx,
          true,
          opts.notificationSender
        ).finally(() => {
          healthPollPending = false;
        });
        const healthPollIntervalMs = Math.max(
          10,
          opts.healthPollIntervalMs ?? 3e4
        );
        const wallClockMs = opts.wallClockMs ?? Date.now;
        const monotonicClockMs = opts.monotonicClockMs ?? (() => performance.now());
        const clockDetector = new ClockJumpDetector(
          opts.clockJumpThresholdMs ?? 12e4
        );
        clockDetector.observe(wallClockMs(), monotonicClockMs());
        const healthMonitor = ctx.guardedAutonomy === void 0 && opts.notificationSender === void 0 ? void 0 : setInterval(() => {
          if (healthPollPending) return;
          healthPollPending = true;
          const clock = clockDetector.observe(
            wallClockMs(),
            monotonicClockMs()
          );
          if (clock.jump_detected) {
            process.stderr.write(
              `osstack: clock jump detected drift_ms=${clock.drift_ms}; re-running external health and recovery probes
`
            );
          }
          guardedRecovery = refreshAutonomyHealthAndRecover(
            ctx,
            clock.jump_detected,
            opts.notificationSender
          ).catch((error) => {
            process.stderr.write(
              `osstack: autonomy recovery monitor failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}
`
            );
          }).finally(() => {
            healthPollPending = false;
          });
        }, healthPollIntervalMs);
        healthMonitor?.unref();
        let shutdownPromise;
        const shutdown = async () => {
          checkpointClosed = true;
          if (checkpointService) {
            try {
              (await checkpointService).close();
            } catch {
            }
          }
          if (shutdownPromise !== void 0) return await shutdownPromise;
          process.off("SIGTERM", onSig);
          process.off("SIGINT", onSig);
          stopUpdateChecker();
          if (healthMonitor !== void 0) clearInterval(healthMonitor);
          shutdownPromise = (async () => {
            ctx.draining = true;
            if (graphRuntime) {
              try {
                await (await graphRuntime).close();
              } catch {
              }
            }
            const collaboratorStopped = ctx.collaborator?.stop();
            for (const ac of ctx.activeRuns.values()) ac.abort();
            for (const session of ctx.loopSessions.values()) {
              session.shutdownRequested = true;
              session.abort.abort();
            }
            for (const session of ctx.dailySessions.values()) {
              session.shutdownRequested = true;
              session.abort.abort();
            }
            const loopCompletions = Array.from(ctx.loopSessions.values()).map((session) => session.done).filter((done) => done !== void 0);
            const dailyCompletions = Array.from(ctx.dailySessions.values()).map((session) => session.done).filter((done) => done !== void 0);
            await Promise.allSettled([
              ...loopCompletions,
              ...dailyCompletions,
              guardedRecovery,
              collaboratorStopped
            ]);
            await new Promise((resolveClose) => server.close(() => resolveClose()));
            if (dashboard !== null) await dashboard.close();
            try {
              ctx.db.close();
            } catch {
            }
            try {
              cleanupStaleSocket(socketPath);
            } catch {
            }
            try {
              releaseLock();
            } catch {
            }
            await shutdownOtel();
          })();
          return await shutdownPromise;
        };
        const onSig = () => {
          void shutdown().then(() => process.exit(0));
        };
        process.once("SIGTERM", onSig);
        process.once("SIGINT", onSig);
        ctx.requestShutdown = onSig;
        const dashOpts = dashboardUrlFromEnv();
        void startDashboardServer(
          () => createDashboardSnapshot({
            db: ctx.db,
            kanban: ctx.kanban,
            dbPath: ctx.dbPath,
            version: DAEMON_VERSION,
            phase: DAEMON_PHASE,
            dashboardUrl: ctx.dashboardUrl ?? dashboardUrl(dashOpts),
            adapters: Array.from(ctx.adapters.values()).map((a) => ({
              id: a.id,
              display_name: a.display_name,
              cost_tier: a.cost_tier
            })),
            activeRuns: ctx.activeRuns.size,
            activeLoops: activeLoopCount(ctx)
          }),
          dashOpts
        ).then((handle2) => {
          dashboard = handle2;
          ctx.dashboardUrl = handle2.url;
        }).catch((err) => {
          process.stderr.write(`osstack: dashboard server failed: ${err.message}
`);
        });
        ctx.collaborator?.recover();
        resolve4({
          close: shutdown,
          socketPath,
          dbPath: ctx.dbPath,
          executeGraphCheckpoint: async (connection, request2) => (await getCheckpoints()).execute(connection, request2),
          graphActionBroker: getBroker,
          graphRuntime: (configuration2) => {
            if (checkpointClosed) return Promise.reject(new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_CLOSED"));
            if (graphRuntime) return Promise.reject(new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_ALREADY_CONFIGURED"));
            const fixed = structuredClone(configuration2);
            return graphRuntime = Promise.all([Promise.resolve().then(() => (init_graph_runtime(), graph_runtime_exports)), getBroker(), getCheckpoints()]).then(async ([{ GraphRuntime: GraphRuntime2 }, broker2, checkpoints]) => {
              const runtime2 = new GraphRuntime2({ ...fixed, db: ctx.db, broker: broker2, checkpoints });
              if (checkpointClosed) {
                await runtime2.close();
                throw new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_CLOSED");
              }
              return runtime2;
            });
          },
          acquireGraphAdmission: async (identity, input) => {
            const trusted = opts.graphIdentities?.get(identity);
            if (!trusted || typeof trusted.authorize !== "function") throw new Error("trusted graph identity and authorization required");
            return admissionFor(ctx).acquire({
              ...input,
              kind: "write",
              workspacePath: trusted.workspacePath,
              authorize: async () => {
                const decision = await makeRepositoryPolicyGate(trusted.workspacePath, true)({ workspacePath: trusted.workspacePath });
                if (!decision.allow) throw new Error(decision.reason);
                await trusted.authorize();
              }
            });
          }
        });
      });
    }).catch(reject);
  });
}

  };

var fence2 = () => {
  const r = current();
  return { scope: r.scope, graphRunId: r.graphRunId, identity: r.identity, expectedGeneration: r.generation, expectedRevision: r.revision, expectedOwner: r.leaseOwner, now: Date.now() };
};
var inspect = async () => ({
  run: current(),
  actions: db.prepare("SELECT node_id,state,execution_ref,outcome_json FROM graph_actions WHERE graph_run_id = ? ORDER BY created_at").all(run.graphRunId),
  budget: (() => {
    const row = db.prepare("SELECT effect_key FROM graph_actions WHERE graph_run_id = ? LIMIT 1").get(run.graphRunId);
    return row ? new GraphActionStore2(db).get(fence2(), row.effect_key).budget : { actionStarts: 0 };
  })(),
  external: existsSync8(external) ? readFileSync8(external, "utf8") : "",
  reentries: existsSync8(reentries) ? readFileSync8(reentries, "utf8") : "",
  checkpoints: db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM graph_checkpoints WHERE graph_run_id = ?").get(run.graphRunId),
  writes: db.prepare("SELECT channel,value FROM graph_checkpoint_writes WHERE graph_run_id = ?").all(run.graphRunId),
  lock: await inspectRepoWriteLock(join9(canonical3.commonDir, "osstack/writer-locks"), canonical3.repoId),
  stopped
});
function recovery() {
  const key = {};
  const fixed = current();
  const verifyWorker = async () => {
    const exit = stopped?.worker;
    if (!exit || exit.kind !== "exited" || !stopped?.actionsSettled || exit.graphRunId !== fixed.graphRunId || exit.generation !== fixed.generation || exit.owner !== fixed.leaseOwner) throw new Error("NO_OBSERVED_EXIT");
    if (handle && JSON.stringify(runtime.observedExit(handle.connection)) !== JSON.stringify(exit)) throw new Error("EXIT_RECEIPT_MISMATCH");
    return { kind: "stopped", graphRunId: exit.graphRunId, generation: exit.generation, owner: exit.owner, confirmedAt: exit.confirmedAt, evidenceRef: exit.evidenceRef };
  };
  runtime.reconciler.register(key, {
    binding: { scope: fixed.scope, graphRunId: fixed.graphRunId, identity: fixed.identity, generation: fixed.generation, owner: fixed.leaseOwner },
    authorize: () => {
    },
    stopWorker: async () => stopped,
    verifyWorker,
    verifyQuiescence: async () => {
      assertProducer();
      durable(join9(root, "quarantine.json"), { repo: canonical3.repoId, runId: fixed.graphRunId, workspace: repo, retained: true });
      return { executorEvidenceRef: "g04-stopped", workspaceEvidenceRef: "retained-test-workspace" };
    },
    verifyOutcome: async () => {
      if (verdictMode === "not_applied" && (existsSync8(external) || readFileSync8(join9(repo, "file.txt"), "utf8") !== "before\n")) throw new Error("ABSENCE_UNPROVEN");
      if (verdictMode === "applied" && (!existsSync8(external) || readFileSync8(external, "utf8") !== "once\n")) throw new Error("APPLIED_UNPROVEN");
      return verdictMode === "unknown" ? { outcome: "unknown", evidenceRef: "still-unknown", observedTokens: 0 } : verdictMode === "not_applied" ? { outcome: "not_applied", evidenceRef: "verified-absent", actualTokens: 0, error: null } : { outcome: "applied", evidenceRef: "implementation-receipt", actualTokens: 0, result: { schemaVersion: 1, kind: "implementation_complete", workspaceLeaseRef: "workspace", receiptRef: "implementation-receipt" } };
    },
    recheck: () => {
      assertProducer();
      if (!stopped?.actionsSettled || stopped.worker.kind !== "exited") throw new Error("NOT_QUIESCENT");
    },
    release: async (result, reverify) => {
      if (releaseFails) throw new Error("TEST_RELEASE_FAILURE");
      if (result.action) await broker.releaseReconciled(result.action.effectKey, reverify);
    }
  });
  return key;
}
if (existsSync8(join9(root, "stopped.json"))) stopped = JSON.parse(readFileSync8(join9(root, "stopped.json"), "utf8"));
send2({ event: "ready", pid: process.pid, run, dbPath: db.name });
process.on("message", async (message) => {
  try {
    let value;
    if (message.op === "start") {
      fault = message.fault ?? "";
      if (run.generation === 0) run = runtime.claim(run, `owner:${process.pid}`, Date.now() + 6e4);
      handle = runtime.start({ request, run, programId: "c5-chain", registration: registration(), authorize: () => {
      }, ...run.generation > 1 ? { resume: { kind: "continue" } } : {} });
      void handle.result.then((result) => send2({ event: "result", result }), (error) => send2({ event: "result-error", error: String(error), logs: handle?.logs() }));
      await handle.ready;
      value = { run };
    } else if (message.op === "stop") {
      const stopPromise = handle.stop();
      held?.();
      held = void 0;
      stopped = await stopPromise;
      durable(join9(root, "stopped.json"), stopped);
      value = stopped;
    } else if (message.op === "inspect") value = await inspect();
    else if (message.op === "singleton") {
      if (!daemon) throw new Error("daemon required");
      value = await daemon.graphRuntime(configuration);
    } else if (message.op === "old-action") {
      value = await broker.handle(handle.connection, { schemaVersion: 1, kind: "worker_action", requestId: "old", scope: run.scope, graphRunId: run.graphRunId, specHash: run.identity.specHash, generation: handle.binding.generation, epoch: 0, nodeId: "implement", effectSlot: "invoke", action: { kind: "implement", inputArtifactRef: "input", workspaceLeaseRef: "workspace" } });
    } else if (message.op === "takeover") {
      const result = await runtime.reconciler.reconcile(recovery(), { kind: "takeover", fence: fence2(), receiptId: `takeover:${run.generation}`, owner: `owner:${process.pid}:${run.generation + 1}`, leaseExpiresAt: Date.now() + 6e4 });
      run = result.result.run;
      value = result;
    } else if (message.op === "reconcile") {
      verdictMode = message.verdict;
      releaseFails = message.releaseFails ?? false;
      const action = db.prepare("SELECT effect_key FROM graph_actions WHERE graph_run_id = ? AND node_id = 'implement'").get(run.graphRunId);
      value = await runtime.reconciler.reconcile(recovery(), { kind: "effect", fence: fence2(), receiptId: message.receiptId, effectKey: action.effect_key });
    } else if (message.op === "verify") {
      const row = db.prepare("SELECT outcome_json FROM graph_actions WHERE graph_run_id = ? AND node_id = 'freeze'").get(run.graphRunId);
      const result = JSON.parse(row.outcome_json).result;
      const manifest = await artifacts.verify(result.artifactDigest);
      const restored = await artifacts.restore(result.artifactDigest, { rootPath: root, directoryName: `restored-${Date.now()}` });
      value = { manifest, restored, content: readFileSync8(join9(restored.worktreePath, "file.txt"), "utf8") };
    } else if (message.op === "close") {
      await runtime.close();
      if (daemon) await daemon.close();
      db.close();
      send2({ id: message.id, value: true });
      process.exit(0);
    } else throw new Error("unknown fixture command");
    send2({ id: message.id, value });
  } catch (error) {
    send2({ id: message.id, error: String(error), stack: error.stack });
  }
});
